**Hamilton**：*（Following and laughing.）* Toddle? With that foot?

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：杰斐逊先生，我在这件事上的决定，同您和门罗参议员对我那法案的决定一样，断无更改。*（门罗与杰斐逊交换眼色，汉密尔顿瞧在眼里）* 失陪一下。我确乎听见将军在唤了。*（走向中间的窗）* 岳父，夜里的寒气于您的痛风不利，您还是早些回吧。

**斯凯勒**：二位先生，晚间好。

**杰斐逊**：晚间好，斯凯勒公民。

**门罗**：晚间好。

**斯凯勒**：*（横穿过去，走向右上的门）* 不搅扰你们了。我这就一步一步蹭回去。

**汉密尔顿**：*（跟在后面笑）* 蹭回去？就凭那只脚？

===Original===

**Schuyler**：*（Throwing open the door up R.）* The foot’s better, Alexander. Good night, gentlemen.

**Hamilton**：Excuse me while I see the General to the door.

**Jefferson**：*（Coming toward C.）* What do you make of that last remark of his﻿—as irrevocable as our decision regarding his bill?

**Monroe**：*（R. C.）* Sounds like an invitation to strike a bargain.

**Jefferson**：Is it worth it?

**Monroe**：We can’t do without him, damn him.

===Chinese===

**斯凯勒**：*（一把推开右上的门）* 脚好多啦，亚历山大。二位先生，晚安。

**汉密尔顿**：恕我失陪，送将军到门口。

**杰斐逊**：*（走向台中）* 他末了那句话，你品出什么味道没有——同我们对那法案的决定一样，断无更改？

**门罗**：*（台中偏右）* 听着倒像邀人做一笔交易。

**杰斐逊**：值得吗？

**门罗**：少了他又办不成，真见他的鬼。

===Original===

**Jefferson**：Shall we support his bill in return for the Capital?

**Monroe**：It’s worth anything to get the Capital away from the North.

**Jefferson**：It will be hard to explain this change of front to the people. I’ve said so much about State Rights.

**Monroe**：You can make some excuse.

**Jefferson**：It will be difficult to explain away.

**Monroe**：*（Chuckling and coming to C.）* Tom Jefferson, you can explain away anything. Give you pen and ink and there isn’t your equal for that in the universe. Besides, we may not have to give up a thing. *（Moving away to R. a little.）*

===Chinese===

**杰斐逊**：那就以支持他的法案，换首都？

**门罗**：只要把首都从北方手里夺过来，出什么价都值。

**杰斐逊**：这一转向，很难向民众交代。州权的话，我说得太多了。

**门罗**：您总能找得出说辞。

**杰斐逊**：只怕辩解不掉。

**门罗**：*（暗笑着走到台中）* 汤姆·杰斐逊，天下就没有你辩解不掉的事。给你纸笔，宇宙之内再无对手。再说，咱们兴许一样东西也不必舍。*（稍稍向右挪开）*

===Original===

**Jefferson**：What do you mean?

**Monroe**：Well, Giles of Virginia has some scheme on. He said if Hamilton got us into a corner﻿—

**Jefferson**：Monroe, Giles is a rascal, and I’ll not be identified with any of his underhand schemes.

**Monroe**：Neither will I. I’ll just leave him alone and trust in Providence.

**Hamilton**：*（Coming down R. of table C.）* Forgive me for leaving you, although I’m afraid, gentlemen, our interview is at an end.

**Jefferson**：*（Advancing a little to **Hamilton**.）* Citizen Hamilton, I have been credited with being a diplomatist.

===Chinese===

**杰斐逊**：什么意思？

**门罗**：嗐，弗吉尼亚的贾尔斯正张罗着一桩算计。他说，汉密尔顿要是把咱们逼到墙角——

**杰斐逊**：门罗，贾尔斯是个无赖，他的阴招我一样也不沾。

**门罗**：我也不沾。由他去，咱们只管信天意。

**汉密尔顿**：*（走到中桌右侧）* 恕我失陪了这么久。不过恕我直言，二位先生，今晚的会谈怕是到此为止了。

**杰斐逊**：*（向汉密尔顿略进一步）* 汉密尔顿公民，世人都算我是个外交家。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：Your valued service, as Minister to France, places that beyond dispute, sir.

**Jefferson**：And I find that, in settling arguments of all kinds, it is necessary to give and take.

**Hamilton**：The best diplomatist, I presume, being the man who gives the least and takes the most.

**Jefferson**：In this instance, Citizen Monroe and myself are prepared to give a great deal, in order that the Southern States shall not be overlooked.

**Monroe**：Treated with contempt.

**Jefferson**：With regard to the Capital, I have a proposal to make. *（**Hamilton** nods his head attentively and comes down in front of sofa and turns to them.）* As we cannot agree upon the selection of a city, why not build us a new city﻿—clean and new and full of the ideals of Liberty and Fraternity! Why choose a city like Albany or New York, marred with the scars of the British tyrant﻿—bristling with the memories of our servitude?

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：您出使法兰西的功绩，早令此说无可置辩，先生。

**杰斐逊**：而依我的经验，了结各种争执，总得有予有取。

**汉密尔顿**：顶好的外交家，想必就是给得最少、取得最多的那位了。

**杰斐逊**：眼下这件事，为了南方各州不至遭人怠慢——

**门罗**：不至遭人作践。

**杰斐逊**：至于首都，我有一项提议。*（汉密尔顿专注地颔首，走到沙发前，回身面向二人）* 既是选不出一座众口归一的城市，何不为我们新建一座——又洁净又崭新，盛满自由与博爱的理想！何必挑奥尔巴尼、纽约那样的城，遍体留着英国暴君的疤痕——处处戳着我们做奴隶的记忆？

===Original===

**Hamilton**：Certainly an original idea. A new city. On the Hudson!

**Monroe**：*（Quickly and emphatically.）* No, not on the Hudson.

**Jefferson**：On the Potomac﻿—halfway between the North and the South!

**Hamilton**：I regret to have to refuse you, Mr. Jefferson, but as I said my decision is irrevocable.

**Jefferson**：I think you said as irrevocable as our decision regarding your bill.

**Hamilton**：*（Turning and apparently trying to recall it.）* Did I? I think I did.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：果真是别开生面的主意。一座新城。就建在哈得孙河上！

**门罗**：*（急切而斩钉截铁）* 不，不在哈得孙河。

**杰斐逊**：建在波托马克河上——南北正中间！

**汉密尔顿**：杰斐逊先生，我很遗憾不得不回绝您。不过我说过，我的决定断无更改。

**杰斐逊**：您似乎说过——同我们对您那法案的决定一样，断无更改。

**汉密尔顿**：*（转过身，作苦苦追忆状）* 我说过吗？好像说过。

===Original===

**Jefferson**：Suppose we﻿—make﻿—a﻿—concession.

**Hamilton**：*（Coming back to **Jefferson**.）* Strike a bargain, do you mean?

**Jefferson**：Well, I wouldn’t care to use that word, Mr. Hamilton.

**Hamilton**：No? We won’t use it, then. We’ll call it concession. *（Moves to front of sofa again.）*

**Jefferson**：Suppose we pass your Bill in return for the Capital?

**Hamilton**：*（With an assumption of astonishment.）* Why, gentlemen, this is a surprising proposal. I fear I must have time to think it over.

===Chinese===

**杰斐逊**：假如我们——作——一项——让步。

**汉密尔顿**：*（回到杰斐逊跟前）* 您是说，做一笔交易？

**杰斐逊**：呃，这个词嘛，我不愿用，汉密尔顿先生。

**汉密尔顿**：是吗？那我们就不用它。就管它叫让步。*（又挪到沙发前）*

**杰斐逊**：假如我们通过您的法案，以首都为报，如何？

**汉密尔顿**：*（故作惊愕）* 哎呀，二位先生，这提议来得太突然。恐怕我得些时日斟酌斟酌。

===Original===

**Monroe**：*（Beside **Jefferson**.）* You’re a quick thinker when you like, Hamilton. *（Knock ready.）*

**Hamilton**：Yes, when I like the proposal. But I am afraid I am getting the worst of the bargain﻿—*（**Jefferson** gives slight movement）* er﻿—concession.

**Jefferson**：We’re offering you something you’ve been fighting for for years.

**Hamilton**：You will admit that yours would be the spectacular victory. The Capital wrested from the North!

**Jefferson**：I do not care for popularity. I am thinking only of what is best for the greatest number.

**Hamilton**：You need my answer now? Can you not give me a week to think it over?

===Chinese===

**门罗**：*（在杰斐逊身旁）* 只要您愿意，您的脑子转得快着呢，汉密尔顿。*（正待敲门）*

**汉密尔顿**：是啊，碰到我中意的提议就快。只是这桩交易里，只怕吃亏的是我——*（杰斐逊微微一动）* 呃——让步。

**杰斐逊**：我们奉上的，是你争了多年的东西。

**汉密尔顿**：可你们得承认，赢得风光的是你们。从北方手里夺走了首都！

**杰斐逊**：我不求众望。我心里想的，只是对最大多数人顶好的安排。

**汉密尔顿**：现在就要我的答复？不能给我一个星期斟酌？

===Original===

**Monroe**：A week! Good Lord, Hamilton.

**Hamilton**：Three days!

**Monroe**：This must be decided now.

**Hamilton**：*（Rising.）* Very well, gentlemen. I agree.

**Monroe**：*（With alacrity.）* Shall we put it in writing?

**Jefferson**：I think our oral pledge will be sufficient.

===Chinese===

**门罗**：一个星期！老天爷，汉密尔顿。

**汉密尔顿**：三天！

**门罗**：非得现在定。

**汉密尔顿**：*（起身）* 好吧，二位先生。我答应。

**门罗**：*（兴冲冲地）* 要不要立个字据？

**杰斐逊**：我想，口头承诺尽够了。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：Your word is your bond. In fact I would rather take your word, gentlemen, than the bond of any state in the Union.

**Monroe**：That’s a bargain then.

**Jefferson**：*（Between them and smiling.）* Really, I cannot permit﻿—

**Hamilton**：Let us say, a final settlement of our difference of opinion. May I offer you some refreshment?

**Jefferson**：Thank you, no. Citizen Monroe will agree that we have already detained you too long.

**Zekiel**：Coun’ Tallyrand! *（Enter **Tallyrand**.）*

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：二位一言，重于债券。说实在的，比起合众国任何一州的债券，我倒更乐意收二位的一句话。

**门罗**：那这就成交了。

**杰斐逊**：*（立于两人之间，微笑）* 真是的，二位这话我可不能答应——

**汉密尔顿**：不妨说，这是我们分歧的最终了断。可要些茶点？

**杰斐逊**：谢谢，不必。门罗公民也会同意，我们已经耽搁您太久了。

**泽基尔**：塔列朗伯爵到！*（塔列朗上）*

===Original===

**Tallyrand**：*（Shaking hands with **Hamilton** and then retiring a little on the right up stage near door.）* My dear Hamilton﻿—oh, a thousand pardons. I see you have the Citizens with you. I intrude.

**Jefferson**：We were about to take our leave. *（Shaking hands with **Hamilton**.）* Good night, Citizen Hamilton. *（Passing to door.）* We have already made our adieux to﻿—

**Tallyrand**：*（Politely.）* Citizen Tallyrand.

**Monroe**：*（Shaking hands with **Hamilton**.）* Good night, Hamilton. *（Goes to room door and turns.）* That was a damned good proposition of yours.

**Hamilton**：*（At room door L. of **Monroe**.）* Oh, pardon me, gentlemen. The proposition came from you.

**Monroe**：*（Coughing uncomfortably.）* Er﻿—yes﻿—

===Chinese===

**塔列朗**：*（与汉密尔顿握手，随即退向右上靠门处）* 亲爱的汉密尔顿——哦，千请恕罪。我见您这儿有几位公民在。是我搅扰了。

**杰斐逊**：我们正要告辞。*（与汉密尔顿握手）* 晚安，汉密尔顿公民。*（向门口走去）* 我们已经向——道过别了。

**塔列朗**：*（彬彬有礼地）* 是塔列朗公民。

**门罗**：*（与汉密尔顿握手）* 晚安，汉密尔顿。*（走到房门口，又回身）* 你方才那个提议，可真不赖。

**汉密尔顿**：*（在门罗左侧的房门口）* 哦，恕我直言，二位先生。提议可是出自你们。

**门罗**：*（尴尬地咳嗽）* 呃——是——

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Slamming room door and coming down excitedly, placing his hands on **Tallyrand’s** shoulders.）* Tallyrand, I needed someone to drink a toast with me. Here’s to the government that’s going to pay its debts. *（Picking up decanter.）*

**Tallyrand**：I drink to that because I like to drink, but drinking will not make your government pay.

**Hamilton**：*（Pouring out two glasses.）* It’s going to pay! It’s going to pay!

**Tallyrand**：Who is going to make it?

**Hamilton**：*（Pointing.）* Jefferson and Monroe.

**Tallyrand**：*（Astounded.）* You have reformed them?

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（砰地关上房门，激动地走回来，双手搭上塔列朗的肩）* 塔列朗，我正需要有个人陪我干一杯。来，敬这个就要还债的政府！*（端起酒瓶）*

**塔列朗**：这一杯我喝——因为我爱喝。可光靠喝，你们的政府是不会还债的。

**汉密尔顿**：*（斟了两杯）* 它会还的！它会还的！

**塔列朗**：谁来叫它还？

**汉密尔顿**：*（手一指）* 杰斐逊和门罗。

**塔列朗**：*（骇然）* 您把他们感化过来了？

===Original===

**Hamilton**：I have﻿—and never struck a blow.

**Tallyrand**：*（Taking up glass.）* I drink, then, to Alexander Hamilton﻿—the greatest of them all!

**Hamilton**：*（Taking up glass.）* That gives you an unfair advantage with the wine. If you will substitute the name of George Washington, I will drink with you.

**Tallyrand**：*（Shrugs shoulders.）* George Washington﻿—yes.

**Hamilton**：*（C., holding up glass.）* To George Washington, the first American!

**Tallyrand**：*（R. of **Hamilton**.）* Well, I will drink anyhow.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：感化过来了——而且一拳未动。

**塔列朗**：*（举杯）* 那我这一杯，就敬亚历山大·汉密尔顿——他们中间最伟大的一位！

**汉密尔顿**：*（举杯）* 这么着，你在酒上占了我不公平的便宜。肯把名字换成乔治·华盛顿，我就陪你干。

**塔列朗**：*（耸耸肩）* 乔治·华盛顿——好吧。

**汉密尔顿**：*（台中，举杯）* 敬乔治·华盛顿，第一位美国人！

**塔列朗**：*（在汉密尔顿右边）* 好吧，横竖我都要喝的。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Good-humored.）* You never did appreciate the greatness of my General.

**Tallyrand**：Oh, yes, he has a great big nose.

**Hamilton**：And a great big heart and a great big soul.

**Tallyrand**：But the brain, it is yours. *（**Hamilton** about to interrupt.）* Don’t let’s quarrel over your General. I come to say goodbye.

**Hamilton**：You leave by tonight’s boat?

**Tallyrand**：Yes, or rather it sails in the early hours of the morning.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（好脾气地）* 对于我这位将军的伟大，你从来就识不透。

**塔列朗**：哦，识得，他那只鼻子就大得伟大。

**汉密尔顿**：还有一颗博大的心、一个博大的灵魂。

**塔列朗**：可脑子嘛，是你的。*（汉密尔顿正要插话）* 别为你那位将军伤了和气。我是来道别的。

**汉密尔顿**：搭今晚的船走？

**塔列朗**：是，或者说，船在凌晨时分起锚。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：Then you have plenty of time. *（Taking chair from in front of sofa and sitting R. of C. table.）* Sit down and have supper with me.

**Tallyrand**：*（Moving away to L.）* Oh, no﻿—you Americans take too much food. When you are not doing something else, you are always sitting down to supper.

**Hamilton**：Well, be a good American and sit down then.

**Tallyrand**：*（Extending hand.）* No, no, I come to give you one last embrace. I am in haste. I must leave you. Adieu. *（Giving him his hand.）*

**Hamilton**：*（Still seated.）* Why not stop and gossip? I’ve finished my work for tonight.

**Tallyrand**：To be frank with you, I have to make my adieux to some﻿—er﻿—ladies.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：那你时间宽裕得很。*（从沙发前取过椅子，在中桌右边坐下）* 坐下，陪我吃顿晚饭吧。

**塔列朗**：*（挪向左边）* 哦，不——你们美国人吃得太凶。但凡不干别的，你们就总在坐下吃饭。

**汉密尔顿**：那就好好学个美国人，坐下便是。

**塔列朗**：*（伸出手）* 不，不，我是来同你作最后一个拥抱的。我赶时辰，必须走了。再见。*（把手递给他）*

**汉密尔顿**：*（仍坐着）* 何不留下来叙叙闲话？我今晚的活儿都做完了。

**塔列朗**：跟你说句实话，我还得去向几位——呃——女士辞行。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Rising.）* Oh!

**Tallyrand**：Ah, your American girls! I find them almost more irresistible than the French.

**Hamilton**：*（Laughing.）* And they’re not so far away﻿—eh?

**Tallyrand**：*（L. C.）* Hamilton, you have finished your work for tonight. Why not come with me? If one is working all the day is it not right that one should play at night? *（Coming over to **Hamilton**.）* A little spree, eh?

**Hamilton**：*（Smiling.）* Why﻿—what do you call a little spree?

**Tallyrand**：*（Shrugs.）* Oh-h﻿—a﻿—leetle spree. You are so good, Hamilton, and I love you for it, but I see the boy leap into your eyes when I say little spree.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（起身）* 哦！

**塔列朗**：啊，你们美国的姑娘！我觉着她们简直比法国姑娘还叫人招架不住。

**汉密尔顿**：*（笑）* 而且路也不算远——嗯？

**塔列朗**：*（台中偏左）* 汉密尔顿，你今晚的活儿做完了。何不跟我同去？一个人白日里操劳一整天，夜里寻个快活，岂不正该如此？*（走到汉密尔顿跟前）* 小小的乐一乐，嗯？

**汉密尔顿**：*（微笑）* 哦——你所谓的小小乐一乐，是指什么？

**塔列朗**：*（耸肩）* 哦——就是——小小的乐一乐嘛。你这个人太方正了，汉密尔顿，我爱的就是这个。可我一说乐一乐，就瞧见个小伙子蹦进你眼睛里去了。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Laughing and moving away to R.）* Do you? By Jove, I believe I deserve it!

**Tallyrand**：We are all of us human﻿—except General Washington.

**Hamilton**：*（Protesting.）* Tallyrand!

**Tallyrand**：A little spree will do you good.

**Hamilton**：*（Crossing to table C. and picks up **Betsy’s** letter.）* No, I think not. I think I’ll stay at home.

**Tallyrand**：Oh, yes, I know﻿—Mrs. Hamilton is charming﻿—*Je l’adore*, but she is away﻿—she is away so long.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（笑着挪向右边）* 是吗？老天在上，我看我也确实该乐一乐了！

**塔列朗**：我们个个都是凡人——只有华盛顿将军除外。

**汉密尔顿**：*（抗议）* 塔列朗！

**塔列朗**：小小的乐一乐，于你有好处。

**汉密尔顿**：*（走到中桌旁，拿起贝特西的信）* 不，我想还是免了。我想我还是待在家里。

**塔列朗**：哦，是了，我知道——汉密尔顿夫人真迷人——Je l'adore，我倾慕她。可她在远方——她走了这么久这么久。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Coming to **Tallyrand** and pulling himself together.）* No, no, no.

**Tallyrand**：I cannot tempt you?

**Hamilton**：I’m afraid you might. That’s why I say goodbye. *（Takes hand.）* Sail home as fast as a fair wind will carry you. Your genius is universal, but your morals are indigenous to Paris. Godspeed!

**Tallyrand**：*（Turning at room door. **Hamilton** is on his left.）* Au ’voir, dear friend. When I am in France and I think of America one big figure will come before my eyes﻿—Alexander Hamilton! The man who makes the fortune of a nation in order to get a living for his family. Au revoir.

**Hamilton**：*（Waving to him.）* Au revoir. Bon voyage. *（Comes into the room.）* Bolt the door, Zekiel; we’ve had enough for tonight. *（**Zekiel** bolts and chains street door, then enters room, closing room door. **Hamilton** walks to his table and picks up Bill.）* Victory! Well, now, we’ll go to bed, Uncle.

**Zekiel**：*（At room door.）* Fo’ de Lawd’s sake, Marse Cunnel, ain’ yo’ goin’ to eat no suppah?

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（走到塔列朗跟前，定住心神）* 不，不，不。

**塔列朗**：我引不动你？

**汉密尔顿**：只怕你引得动。所以我才要说再见。*（握住他的手）* 趁着顺风，尽快扬帆回家去吧。你的天才是普世的，你的德行却只长在巴黎的土壤里。一路顺风！

**塔列朗**：*（在房门口回身。汉密尔顿在他左边）* 再见，亲爱的朋友。日后我在法兰西想起美国，眼前总会浮现出一个高大的身影——亚历山大·汉密尔顿！那位为了一家人糊口，却挣下一个国家家业的人。再见。

**汉密尔顿**：*（向他挥手）* 再见。一路顺风。*（回到屋里）* 把门闩上，泽基尔；今晚咱们够热闹的了。*（泽基尔闩好临街大门，挂上链子，随后进屋关上房门。汉密尔顿走到桌前，拿起法案）* 胜利了！好了，这下咱们睡觉去，大叔。

**泽基尔**：*（在房门口）* 老天爷哎，上校老爷，您那晚饭还吃不吃啦？

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Remembering. Shuts drawer and comes to behind table C.）* Supper? Yes.

**Zekiel**：*（Coming down to R. corner of table C.）* That’s ’bout the fourth suppah I brought in this night, and yo’ ain’ goin’ t’ eat it.

**Hamilton**：I’m going to eat all that supper, and I’m going to finish this bottle of wine. *（Picking up decanter.）*

**Zekiel**：Don’ yo’ drink it on an empty stomach; it’ll go straight to yo’ haid. An’ dere’s noder bottle ain’ been touched, an’ de corkscrew a-lyin’ close ’longside.

**Hamilton**：Zekiel, there’s a conspiracy amongst you to lead me into temptation. *（Goes up to escritoire in L. corner, sits, and is putting away papers.）* You go to bed.

**Zekiel**：Yes, Marse Hamilton. Yessah.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（想起来似的。关上抽屉，走到中桌后）* 晚饭？吃的。

**泽基尔**：*（走到中桌右角）* 这都今儿晚上第四回端饭进来了，您又是不吃的。

**汉密尔顿**：那顿饭我这就吃，这瓶酒也要喝干。*（端起酒瓶）*

**泽基尔**：您可别空着肚子喝；那是直冲脑门的。还有一瓶没动呢，起子就搁在旁边。

**汉密尔顿**：泽基尔，你们这是合起伙来引我犯罪。*（走到左角的写字台前坐下，收拾文件）* 你睡觉去。

**泽基尔**：是，汉密尔顿老爷。是，老爷。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：What is it, Zekiel? What’s the matter?

**Zekiel**：*（Giggling.）* ‘Scuse me, Marse Hamilton, but I jes’ couldn’t help heah what Cunnel Coun’ Tallyran’ say to yo’ ’bout goin’ on a little spree.

**Hamilton**：Ah, you mustn’t be shocked, Uncle. He didn’t mean it.

**Zekiel**：Law, no, I ain’ shocked! I think him right, Mars’ Ham’ton, quite right.

**Hamilton**：What?

**Zekiel**：*（Working to door up R. at back.）* Yo’-all been stickin’ too close to wo’k. Leetle spree do yo’ sight ob good.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：怎么了，泽基尔？什么事？

**泽基尔**：*（嘿嘿直笑）* 恕我多嘴，汉密尔顿老爷，塔列朗伯爵上校跟您说的那档子乐一乐的事，我实在没忍住，听了去。

**汉密尔顿**：啊，你可别吓着，大叔。他不过随口一说。

**泽基尔**：嗐，哪能呢，我才不吓着呢！我倒觉得他说得对，汉密尔顿老爷，对得很。

**汉密尔顿**：什么？

**泽基尔**：*（一边做事一边往后右上的门去）* 您成天价贴在活儿上，贴得太紧了。小小的乐一乐，保管对您大有好处。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：Uncle!

**Zekiel**：If I been a-wo’kin’ night an’ day, same’s yo’ been a-doin’, reckon I’d lak a leetle spree myself.

**Hamilton**：Why, you black rascal, at your age!

**Zekiel**：Well, sah, dat am de on’y thing ’at ’ud pavent it, Marse Cunnel.

**Hamilton**：*（Laughing and going quickly toward door R.; shouts.）* Get off to bed!

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Outside.）* Is this Alexander Hamilton’s house?

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：大叔！

**泽基尔**：我要是也像您这样没白没黑地干，想想自己也想乐一乐呢。

**汉密尔顿**：嗬，你这个黑老家伙，就凭你这把年纪！

**泽基尔**：嗐，老爷，也就是这把年纪，才拦得住我喽，上校老爷。

**汉密尔顿**：*（笑着快步走向右侧门，扬声喊）* 快睡觉去！

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（在外面）* 这是亚历山大·汉密尔顿先生府上吗？

===Original===

**Hamilton**：Yes, this is Mr. Hamilton’s house.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Is he at home?

**Hamilton**：Yes, he is at home. What do you want?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：I want to see him.

**Hamilton**：Won’t it do tomorrow?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Oh, no. I must see him tonight.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：是，这里是汉密尔顿先生的府上。

**雷诺兹夫人**：他在家吗？

**汉密尔顿**：在，他在家。您有什么事？

**雷诺兹夫人**：我想见他。

**汉密尔顿**：明天不成吗？

**雷诺兹夫人**：哦，不成。我今晚必须见到他。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：Come in.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：May I see him?

**Hamilton**：I am Mr. Hamilton.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Wide-eyed.）* Oh-h﻿—are you Alexander Hamilton? I thought he was quite old. Why, you’re young, aren’t you?

**Hamilton**：*（Smiling.）* Not very young.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Why, you *are*. You look quite boyish. It’s very late, isn’t it?

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：请进。

**雷诺兹夫人**：我可以见他吗？

**汉密尔顿**：我就是汉密尔顿先生。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（睁大了眼睛）* 哦——您就是亚历山大·汉密尔顿？我还当他上了年纪了呢。哎呀，您这么年轻，不是吗？

**汉密尔顿**：*（微笑）* 也算不上很年轻。

**雷诺兹夫人**：哎呀，就是嘛。您瞧着简直像个后生。时候很晚了，是不是？

===Original===

**Hamilton**：It is rather late for business hours.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Were you just going to bed?

**Hamilton**：I think I was.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Oh, then I ought not to have disturbed you. When I got to your door I thought perhaps it might be too late. That’s why I knocked with my knuckles instead of with the knocker.

**Hamilton**：Yes, I wondered why you did that.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Smiling sweetly.）* Yes, it hurt them too. It was mighty nice of you to let me in.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：要论办公的时辰，确是不早了。

**雷诺兹夫人**：您方才正要睡吗？

**汉密尔顿**：大概是吧。

**雷诺兹夫人**：哦，那我真不该搅扰您。走到您门口那会儿，我就想着怕是太晚了。所以我用指节敲门，没敢动门环。

**汉密尔顿**：是啊，我正纳闷您为何那样敲。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（甜甜一笑）* 是啊，指节还挺疼的呢。您肯放我进来，心肠真好。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Smiling.）* What do you want?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：I hardly like to tell you. *（Moving down a step.）*

**Hamilton**：*（A little nonplussed.）* What’s your name?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Reynolds﻿—my name is.

**Hamilton**：Reynolds?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Yes. I’m related to the Livingstons of New York State.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（微笑）* 您有什么事？

**雷诺兹夫人**：我有点儿难于启齿。*（往下走了一步）*

**汉密尔顿**：*（微微有点窘）* 您贵姓？

**雷诺兹夫人**：雷诺兹——我姓雷诺兹。

**汉密尔顿**：雷诺兹？

**雷诺兹夫人**：是。我同纽约州的利文斯顿家沾着亲。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Warmly.）* Oh!

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Yes. I’ve so often heard of you from the Livingstons.

**Hamilton**：*（Shaking hands with her.）* Yes, the Livingstons are very old friends of mine. Won’t you sit down? *（Indicates chair R. of C. table; she sits demurely but with a “so far so good” look in her eye. He seats himsself on the end of the sofa facing her.）* Well now, what can I do for you?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：I want money.

**Hamilton**：*（Astonished.）* Money? But﻿—why did you come to me?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Innocently.）* Why, I’m an American. I’m in need of money. I thought it the place of the Secretary of the Treasury of my own country to help me.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（热络起来）* 哦！

**雷诺兹夫人**：是啊。利文斯顿家的人常说起您呢。

**汉密尔顿**：*（同她握手）* 是，利文斯顿一家是我多年的老友。您请坐。*（指了指中桌右侧的椅子；她端庄地坐下，眼里却含着一丝「至此还算顺遂」的神情。他在对面沙发的尽头落座。）* 好了，我能为您效什么劳？

**雷诺兹夫人**：我想要钱。

**汉密尔顿**：*（愕然）* 钱？可是——您为何来找我？

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（一脸无辜）* 嗐，我是个美国人啊。我缺钱。我想着，帮帮我，正是敝国财政部长分内的事。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Turning away for a moment amused.）* But, my child, I have no money. Why don’t you go to Robert Livingston? He’s in Philadelphia now.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（With a quick look away.）* Oh, is he? But my people are the Henry Livingstons.

**Hamilton**：Oh!

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Quickly.）* My father was in the Commissary Department during the war.

**Hamilton**：Was he?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Yes. Mr. Lewis, his name was.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（觉着好笑，一时别过脸去）* 可是，孩子，我没有钱。您为何不去找罗伯特·利文斯顿？他如今就在费城。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（眼光飞快地闪开）* 哦，是吗？不过我们家是亨利·利文斯顿那一支。

**汉密尔顿**：哦！

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（忙接口）* 我父亲战争年间在军需部当过差。

**汉密尔顿**：是吗？

**雷诺兹夫人**：是。他姓刘易斯。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Interestedly.）* Well, now, what do you want this money for?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（With a faltering voice.）* I want to run away.

**Hamilton**：To run away! From whom?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：My husband.

**Hamilton**：Your husband? Why, you’re a child!

**Mrs. Reynolds**：I’m twenty-six. I don’t look it, do I? Oh, he’s been nothing to me for a long time, but tonight he came to my lodgings where I’ve been living and he struck me, here. *（Touching her breast.）* So I thought you could give me the money that tomorrow I’d go to New York.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（来了兴致）* 好了，您要这笔钱做什么用？

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（声气发颤）* 我想逃走。

**汉密尔顿**：逃走！躲开谁？

**雷诺兹夫人**：我丈夫。

**汉密尔顿**：您丈夫？哎呀，您还是个孩子呢！

**雷诺兹夫人**：我二十六了。看不出来的，是吧？唉，他早就不拿我当回事了。可今夜他寻到我租住的寓所，打了我，打在这儿。*（掩着胸口）* 所以我想，您若能给我这笔钱，明天我就动身去纽约。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：And tonight?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Oh, I can go back to my lodgings. *（With a Madonna-like simplicity.）* He won’t come there again tonight.

**Hamilton**：*（Leaning forward.）* What did you say your name was?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Mrs. Reynolds﻿—Maria is my first name. At home I was always called Joy.

**Hamilton**：Joy!

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Yes, Joy, but I haven’t had much joy since I married. *（Breaking down.）* Oh, but I want it! I want joy and happiness.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：那今夜呢？

**雷诺兹夫人**：哦，我回寓所去就是。*（带着圣母般的天真）* 今夜他不会再上那儿去了。

**汉密尔顿**：*（俯身向前）* 您方才说您叫什么来着？

**雷诺兹夫人**：雷诺兹夫人——我的闺名是玛丽亚。在家里，人人管我叫乔伊。

**汉密尔顿**：乔伊！

**雷诺兹夫人**：是，乔伊。可打结婚起，我就没过上多少快乐日子。*（情绪决了堤）* 哦，可我多想要啊！我想要快乐，想要幸福。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（After a pause. Rising and moving to R. and not quite knowing what to say to her; she glances at him quickly as he does so.）* Things will look brighter tomorrow. *（Bending over her.）*

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Despondently.）* No, I’ve waited for years for things to be brighter tomorrow.

**Hamilton**：*（Sympathetically.）* I fear you are very tired.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：I am. *（With a side glance at the things on the table.）* I’ve had nothing since morning.

**Hamilton**：Haven’t you? *（Suddenly remembering.）* Why, neither have I! *（Coming down R. a step or two.）*

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Nothing to eat since morning! *（Rising.）* You poor boy! *（Putting her arms on his and then going quickly to back of C. table.）* Why, you’ve got all sorts of things here﻿—and wine.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（沉默片刻。起身走向右边，一时不知对她说什么好；他一动身，她飞快地瞟了他一眼）* 到了明天，光景会亮堂些的。*（俯身向她）*

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（灰心丧气）* 不会的。就盼着「明天光景亮堂些」，我盼了这些年，也没盼来。

**汉密尔顿**：*（怜惜地）* 只怕您实在累坏了。

**雷诺兹夫人**：累坏了。*（斜眼瞟了瞟桌上的东西）* 我打早上起就水米没沾牙。

**汉密尔顿**：是吗？*（蓦然想起）* 哎呀，我也没吃呢！*（往右下来一两步）*

**雷诺兹夫人**：打早上起就没吃东西！*（起身）* 你这可怜的人儿！*（把胳膊搭上他的手臂，随即快步走到中桌后）* 哎呀，你这儿什么都有——还有酒呢。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：Will you have a glass of wine?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（L. of table and above it.）* Oh, yes, I love wine! *（He gives it to her laughing. She drinks.）* Oh, I don’t think I should have had it﻿—I’ve had nothing to eat.

**Hamilton**：In that case, I believe, it goes straight to the head. *（They laugh.）*

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Putting down glass.）* Aren’t you going to have one?

**Hamilton**：Yes, I’m going to have one. Here’s wishing that joy may come back into your life.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Watches him and comes down a little in front of table.）* Do you know what it is to be lonely?

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：您喝一杯酒吧？

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（在桌子左上方）* 哦，好，我爱喝酒！*（他笑着递给她。她饮下）* 哦，我想我本不该喝的——我还什么都没吃呢。

**汉密尔顿**：这样的话，只怕酒劲要直冲头了。*（两人笑）*

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（放下酒杯）* 你不喝一杯吗？

**汉密尔顿**：喝，我这就喝一杯。祝快乐重新回到你的生活里来。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（望着他，走到桌子前侧一点）* 你知道什么叫孤独吗？

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（At R. corner of table.）* I do. I’ve been very lonely sometimes.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Going to him.）* I believe you’re lonely now.

**Hamilton**：I believe I am.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Two lonely people, alone! Have you nobody here to talk to?

**Hamilton**：Nobody﻿—but you. *（Recovering.）* And that is why I am going to send you away. But first, you must have some food. *（Moving to back of C. table.）*

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Gently.）* Oh, no﻿—not if you’re afraid of my staying. I see you think it isn’t right.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（在桌子右角）* 知道。我有时孤独得很呢。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（走到他跟前）* 我看这会儿你就孤独。

**汉密尔顿**：我看也是。

**雷诺兹夫人**：两个孤独的人，同守一处！你在这儿就没有个说话的人吗？

**汉密尔顿**：没有谁——除了你。*（回过神来）* 正因如此，我才要送你走。不过先得让你吃点儿东西。*（走到中桌后）*

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（轻柔地）* 哦，不——既然您怕我留下。我看您是觉得这样不合适。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：I think it’s better not. But you must have some food.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Passing her hand over her brow.）* No, no, thank you. I will go. *（Moving over to R. of him.）*

**Hamilton**：*（Coming down a step.）* But I can’t let you go like this.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：No, I see I was wrong. I had no right to ask you for money.

**Hamilton**：But I don’t like to﻿—

**Mrs. Reynolds**：No, thank you, I’ll go﻿—I﻿—﻿—﻿

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：我想还是不留的好。可你一定得吃点儿东西。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（抬手抚过额角）* 不，不必了，谢谢。我这就走。*（挪到他右边）*

**汉密尔顿**：*（往下一步）* 可我不能眼看你这就走。

**雷诺兹夫人**：是，我知道我错了。我本没有权利向您讨钱。

**汉密尔顿**：可我不忍——

**雷诺兹夫人**：不，谢谢您，我走——我——

===Original===

**Hamilton**：*（Catching her.）* What’s the matter?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Recovering; dazed.）* I don’t know what’s come over me. I feel so hot and funny.

**Hamilton**：Well﻿—*（Looks for a place to seat her.）* Sit here. *（Sits her on sofa.）*

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Looking up at him.）* It must have been the wine. *（They laugh.）*

**Hamilton**：*（Having one knee on sofa, steps over sofa and comes behind her; arranges pillows at head of ditto.）* Rest here a moment.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Reclining and feeling her face.）* Feel my face how it’s burning.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：*（一把扶住她）* 怎么了？

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（缓过神来；晕乎乎地）* 我不知道这是怎么了。只觉得浑身发热，怪怪的。

**汉密尔顿**：好了——*（找个地方让她坐）* 坐这儿。*（扶她在沙发上坐下）*

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（抬眼望着他）* 准是那杯酒闹的。*（两人笑）*

**汉密尔顿**：*（一膝跪上沙发，跨过去绕到她身后；在沙发头上安置靠垫）* 在这儿靠一会儿。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（半躺着，摸自己的脸）* 你摸摸我的脸，烧得多烫。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：I’ll get you some water.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：It’s wonderful to be with someone who is kind to me.

**Hamilton**：Are you feeling better? *（Bending over her.）*

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Subtly getting her fingers into his right hand; he takes the glass in his left.）* Yes, yes. Let me stay﻿—just a moment. *（She holds his hand very unconsciously and rests a moment with eyes closed.）* Now I’ll go.

**Hamilton**：*（Bending low over her, recovers himself with an effort.）* I’ll call my old negro servant. He shall take you home. *（Puts glass on C. table.）*

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Rising quickly, and gathering cloak around her.）* Oh, no, no! What would he think? No, I’ll go, thank you.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：我去给你倒点儿水。

**雷诺兹夫人**：有个待我好的人在身边，这滋味真好。

**汉密尔顿**：好受些了吗？*（俯身向她）*

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（不着痕迹地把手指探进他的右手；他用左手端起酒杯）* 好些了，好些了。让我再待一会儿——就一小会儿。*（她似浑然不觉地握着他的手，合眼歇了片刻）* 这就走。

**汉密尔顿**：*（低低俯在她身上，奋力定住心神）* 我去唤我家老黑人仆人来，让他送你回家。*（把酒杯放到中桌上）*

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（霍地起身，拢起外套）* 哦，不，不！他会怎么想？不必了，我自己走，谢谢您。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：But I can’t let you﻿—*（**Mrs. Reynolds** slightly reels.）* I’ll see you to your door.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（With a triumphant gleam in her eyes.）* Will you? Oh, but, Mr. Hamilton, I couldn’t dream of taking you out at this hour. It’s so late.

**Hamilton**：*（Coming down to her.）* That’s why I can’t let you go alone. *（Feels her thin cloak.）* But I’m afraid you’ll be chilly in the night air. *（Putting his hat on and taking a second cloak from chair R. of bookcase C. at back.）* Here, put this around you.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Cuddling into it, they bump slightly and both laugh.）* Is it one of yours?

**Hamilton**：Yes. *（Laughing.）* And very big for you.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：It’s very cozy.

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：可我不能让你——*（雷诺兹夫人微微一个趔趄）* 我送你到你门口。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（眼里闪过一丝得意的光）* 真的吗？哦，不过，汉密尔顿先生，这样的时辰劳您出门，我做梦也不敢想。太晚啦。

**汉密尔顿**：*（走到她跟前）* 正因太晚，我才更不能让你独自走。*（摸了摸她单薄的外套）* 只是夜气里怕你要着凉。*（戴上帽子，从后部中书柜右边的椅上取来第二件外套）* 来，把这个裹上。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（缩进外套里，两人轻轻撞了一下，都笑了）* 这是您的吗？

**汉密尔顿**：是。*（笑）* 你穿嫌大得很呢。

**雷诺兹夫人**：裹着真暖和。

===Original===

**Hamilton**：Now.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：*（Brightly.）* May take your arm?

**Hamilton**：Why, you’re feeling much better.

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Oh, yes!

**Hamilton**：I think you had better have another glass of wine?

**Mrs. Reynolds**：Oh, no, thank you; just take me home. Isn’t it wonderful to be taken home by Alexander Hamilton?

===Chinese===

**汉密尔顿**：走吧。

**雷诺兹夫人**：*（轻快地）* 可以挽您的胳膊吗？

**汉密尔顿**：哦，你可是好多了。

**雷诺兹夫人**：哦，好多了！

**汉密尔顿**：我看你还是再喝一杯酒吧？

**雷诺兹夫人**：哦，不了，谢谢；送我回家就好。让亚历山大·汉密尔顿送回家——这岂不是天底下顶美妙的事？

## Act III / 第三幕

===Original===
Scene.﻿—Same as in Act II. In October of the same year. There are flowers about, accentuating the feeling that the woman of the house has come back. The furniture is all placed as at the rise of the second act. On the mantelpiece up stage end is a small vase of autumn flowers. On small table above fireplace large bowl of autumn flowers. On bookcase center at back a vase of goldenrod, on bookcase left, a small vase with yellow flowers and on **Hamilton’s** table a glass bowl of violets. Washington’s chair is placed a little further to left. The chair against wall below door left is removed and placed at left of **Hamilton’s** table. The small chair back of sofa is placed under small table at window on right. A chair similar to the other chairs in the room is placed back of sofa. There are fresh lace curtains at windows.

===Chinese===
场景——与第二幕同。同年十月。屋内四处摆着鲜花，愈发衬出女主人家已经回来的气象。家具布置一概与第二幕开场时相同。台后一端的壁炉架上摆着一小瓶秋花。壁炉上方的小桌上一大碗秋花。后部正中的书柜上一瓶一枝黄花，左侧书柜上一只插着黄色小花的小瓶，汉密尔顿的桌上则是一只玻璃碗养的紫罗兰。华盛顿的座椅略向左移。左门下方靠墙的椅子挪到汉密尔顿桌子左侧。沙发后面的小椅子放到右边窗下的小桌底下。沙发后另摆一把与室内其他椅子相仿的椅子。窗上挂着新换的蕾丝窗帘。

===Original===
**Betsy**：*（Laughing.）* Now, Zekiel, catch these curtains when I drop them.

**Zekiel**：I certainly do wish you’d let me come up them steps ’stead ob you, Mis’ Betsy.

**Betsy**：Why, you dear old darky, you stay where you’re safe.

**Zekiel**：Yo’ve come all cross de ocean an’ yo’ still alive. Don’ want nothin’ to happen to yo’ no sooner’n yo’ get on tere firm. When yo’ come home las’ night, I cut’ny was glad to see yo’﻿—an’ Mis’ Angelica, too﻿—and Mars’ Ham’ton has been jumpin’ about lak a schoolboy evah since.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：*（笑着）* 好了，泽基尔，我一撒手，你就把窗帘接住。

**泽基尔**：俺实在盼着您让俺替您上那梯子，贝特西小姐。

**贝特西**：嗐，你这好心的老黑人，你就待在稳妥的地方吧。

**泽基尔**：您漂洋过海一路回来，人还好好的。可不能让您刚一踏上结实的地皮就出点什么事。昨儿夜里您到家，俺那心里别提多高兴了——还有安杰莉卡小姐——打那以后，汉密尔顿老爷就乐得像个学童似的，蹦蹦跳跳个没完。

===Original===
**Betsy**：Now﻿—catch! *（Drops curtain.）* And remember, Zekiel, when you get married and have a house of your own﻿—

**Zekiel**：*（Yapping.）* Me git married! Ha, ha! Yo’ sure is amusin’. Guess if I evah get mar’d, I won’t have nothin’ ob ma own. *（Laughs.）*

**Betsy**：Remember if you have lace curtains﻿ ﻿… you have *clean* ones.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：好——接住！*（撒手放下窗帘）* 记着，泽基尔，等你往后成了家，有了自己的屋子——

**泽基尔**：*（嘻嘻哈哈）* 俺成家！哈，哈！您可真会说笑。真要成了家，只怕连俺自个儿都不归俺自个儿喽。*（笑）*

**贝特西**：记住，屋里要是挂蕾丝窗帘……就得挂*干净*的。

===Original===
**Angelica**：Why, Betsy Hamilton! *（Closing door and to C.）* I called to see if you were up! I see you are!

**Betsy**：*（Up on the ladder.）* Angelica Church, the next time you have scarlet fever, I hope you’ll have it in your own country and not drag me over to England to nurse you. The state of this house is beyond belief. *（Mounting another step.）*

**Angelica**：Betsy, if you’re going to mount any higher, you’d better let *me* come and hold those steps.

**Betsy**：Now, Zekiel, take those curtains and give them to Mary for the wash.

**Zekiel**：I will do that. Mis’ Betsy. *（Moving to door L.）* I cut’ny do hope Mars’ Ham’ton don’ come in an’ fine yo’ riskin’ yo’ life yo’ fust day home.

===Chinese===
**安杰莉卡**：嗬，贝特西·汉密尔顿！*（关上门，走到台中）* 我来瞧瞧你起了没有！看来是起了！

**贝特西**：*（站在梯子上）* 安杰莉卡·丘奇，下回你再得猩红热，拜托在你自己国家里得，别把我拽到英格兰去伺候你。这屋子脏得简直没法相信。*（又上了一级）*

**安杰莉卡**：贝特西，你要是还想再往上爬，顶好让*我*过来扶着梯子。

**贝特西**：好了，泽基尔，把这些窗帘拿去交给玛丽洗。

**泽基尔**：这就去，贝特西小姐。*（走向左侧门）* 俺就怕汉密尔顿老爷这时候进来，瞧见您到家头一天就在玩儿命。

===Original===
**Angelica**：How you can do it, I don’t know. I’ve felt the motion of the boat all night. I wouldn’t do that if you paid me.

**Betsy**：If I could afford to pay you, Angelica, I’d employ somebody who knew how.

**Angelica**：*（Coming down C. a little.）* Where’s Amiable?

**Betsy**：Angelica, I don’t think it’s fair that you should have a pet name for my husband.

===Chinese===
**安杰莉卡**：你怎么爬得上去的，我真不明白。船晃的那股劲儿我到现在还没缓过来。给钱我也不干。

**贝特西**：安杰莉卡，我要是付得起钱，我就雇个会干这个的人了。

**安杰莉卡**：*（向台中走近几步）* 「和气人」在哪儿呢？

**贝特西**：安杰莉卡，你给我丈夫起了个小名，我看这不公平。

===Original===
**Angelica**：Well, I’ve always called him Amiable because it fits him so much better than Alexander. Where is he?

**Betsy**：I sent him to walk three times around the Common, while I took down those curtains.

**Angelica**：He’s a darling. I wonder you can bear to let him out of your sight.

**Betsy**：*（Sitting on top step.）* I can’t, dear, I can’t. Oh, I’m so glad to be home! But when I came in here this morning I couldn’t see anything but those dirty lace curtains, so I had to send him out.

**Angelica**：*（Laughing.）* And then, it’ll be nice to see him come in again, won’t it? *（Sitting on end of sofa.）*

===Chinese===
**安杰莉卡**：嗐，我一直管他叫「和气人」，因为这名字比「亚历山大」贴切多了。他人呢？

**贝特西**：我打发他去公地绕三圈，趁这工夫我好把窗帘卸下来。

**安杰莉卡**：他可真是个宝贝。你竟舍得让他离开你的眼皮底下，真叫我纳闷。

**贝特西**：*（坐在梯子顶上一级）* 舍不得，亲爱的，舍不得。哦，回家来可真好！可今儿早晨我一进这屋子，满眼只见那几幅脏窗帘，别的什么都看不见，只好把他打发出去。

**安杰莉卡**：*（笑）* 再说，回头再看他进来，不也是桩美事吗？*（在沙发一头坐下）*

===Original===
**Betsy**：How do you think he looks?

**Angelica**：Hale and hearty.

**Betsy**：Do you? I thought he was looking quite thin and pale.

**Angelica**：Oh, because you’ve been away, of course. Well, I’m very sorry, but I think he looks fat and well.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：你觉得他气色怎么样？

**安杰莉卡**：硬朗着呢，精神着呢。

**贝特西**：是吗？我倒觉得他看上去又瘦又苍白。

**安杰莉卡**：哦，那自然是因为你出远门去了。好吧，很抱歉，我可觉得他养得白白胖胖、好好的。

===Original===
**Betsy**：I ought not to have left him.

**Angelica**：You’re terribly in love, aren’t you?

**Betsy**：I am, Angelica, and I can’t get over it. You know Alexander is the most wonderful man in the world.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：我不该丢下他的。

**安杰莉卡**：你爱他爱得要命，是不是？

**贝特西**：是的，安杰莉卡，我怎么也爱不够。你是知道的，亚历山大是天底下最了不起的男人。

===Original===
**Angelica**：*（Back to C.）* Is he? What about my husband?

**Betsy**：Oh, well, John Church is a dear old thing, but you couldn’t possibly be *terribly* in love with him, could you? Besides he’s an Englishman.

**Angelica**：What do you mean, Betsy Hamilton? I *am* terribly in love with him.

**Betsy**：I know you are, dear, but you couldn’t possibly love John as I love Alexander.

===Chinese===
**安杰莉卡**：*（回到台中）* 是吗？那我丈夫呢？

**贝特西**：哦，这个嘛，约翰·丘奇是个可亲的老好人，可你总不至于爱他爱得*要命*吧？再说他是英格兰人。

**安杰莉卡**：你这话什么意思，贝特西·汉密尔顿？我*就是*爱他爱得要命。

**贝特西**：我知道你爱他，亲爱的，可你爱约翰，怎么也爱不到我爱亚历山大的那份上。

===Original===
**Angelica**：And why not?

**Betsy**：Well, he’s a dear, but he has no brains﻿—now has he?

**Angelica**：He has very good brains for an Englishman. *（Moves in front of sofa, removing shawl.）* Besides he’s easier to manage that way.

**Betsy**：He’s very nice and he’s very rich, but he isn’t Alexander.

**Angelica**：Well, as I prefer having a husband to myself, it’s just as well he isn’t. *（They both laugh. **Angelica** sits end of sofa. **Betsy** resumes her work.）* Betsy, it’s sheer nonsense, your going on doing all the work in this house as you do.

===Chinese===
**安杰莉卡**：为什么就到不了？

**贝特西**：嗐，他人是可亲，可他没脑子——你说是不是？

**安杰莉卡**：以一个英格兰人来说，他的脑子算很好的了。*（走到沙发前，取下披肩）* 再说，这样也更好拿捏。

**贝特西**：他人很好，也很有钱，可他不是亚历山大。

**安杰莉卡**：嗐，我巴不得独占一个丈夫呢，他不是亚历山大才好。*（两人都笑了。安杰莉卡坐在沙发一头。贝特西继续干活）* 贝特西，你还像这样样样家务都亲自做，简直是胡闹。

===Original===
**Betsy**：I like it.

**Angelica**：You don’t like it, and you know it. A daughter of General Schuyler doing this kind of work﻿—it isn’t right.

**Betsy**：Well, you know Alexander’s salary, don’t you? Three thousand dollars a year.

**Angelica**：Why don’t you let father make you a decent allowance?

**Betsy**：Alexander would rather die than take a penny from anyone.

**Angelica**：Then why don’t you make him give up working for his old country and let him work for himself and for you? *（Rising and crossing to back of table L.）* Father says he could make twenty thousand dollars a year easily as a lawyer in private practice.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：我乐意。

**安杰莉卡**：你才不乐意呢，你自己清楚。斯凯勒将军的千金干这种活计——不像话。

**贝特西**：嗐，亚历山大的薪水你不是知道吗？一年三千美元。

**安杰莉卡**：怎么不让父亲给你一笔像样的贴补？

**贝特西**：亚历山大宁可死，也不肯拿任何人一个子儿。

**安杰莉卡**：那你何不劝他别再给这个国家卖命，让他为自己、为你做事？*（起身，走到左桌后）* 父亲说，他要是自己开业当律师，一年挣两万美元轻轻松松。

===Original===
**Betsy**：*（Dusting panes of glass.）* The life of this Republic is dearer to him than anything else in the world.

**Angelica**：Oh! Dearer than his wife and family, I suppose?

**Betsy**：There’s no question of that. I know his ambitions and I’m proud to be helping﻿—if it’s only in this way.

**Angelica**：*（Throwing down paper disgustedly.）* Three thousand dollars a year! How do they expect him to provide for you? The government ought to be ashamed of itself. *（Shakes steps.）*

**Betsy**：*（Balancing herself on the top step.）* If you get so excited at the bottom of these steps, I shan’t need anybody to provide for me.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：*（掸着窗玻璃）* 在他心里，这个共和国的性命比世上什么都贵重。

**安杰莉卡**：哦！比老婆孩子还贵重，我猜是吧？

**贝特西**：那没有什么可争的。我知道他的抱负，能帮上忙——哪怕只是这样帮——我也觉得光荣。

**安杰莉卡**：*（厌烦地把报纸一摔）* 一年三千美元！他们指望他拿什么养活你？政府真该为自己害臊。*（摇了摇梯子）*

**贝特西**：*（在梯子顶上稳住身子）* 你要老在梯子底下这么激动，往后也就不用谁来养活我了。

===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（Slamming door behind him.）* Betsy Schuyler! Come down at once. What on earth are you doing up there?

**Betsy**：*（Beaming at him.）* I wanted to get a good view of you as you came in, dear.

**Hamilton**：*（Putting stick against wall R. of bookcase at C. back.）* Come down, madam, immediately, or I’ll come up and fetch you. *（Putting hat on bookcase.）*

**Betsy**：*（Extending both arms invitingly.）* I dare you to kiss me. *（He dashes for the steps.）*

**Angelica**：*（Intercepting him at R. of ladder.）* Now, if there’s any kissing to be done, you start at the bottom of the ladder. *（She kisses him.）* I’ve left my husband in England, and I’m starving to death. *（Kisses him again.）* Good morning, Amiable.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（砰地带上身后的门）* 贝特西·斯凯勒！马上下来。你到底在上头干什么？

**贝特西**：*（笑吟吟地望着他）* 我想在你进门的时候，好好把你瞧个全乎，亲爱的。

**汉密尔顿**：*（把手杖靠在后部正中书柜右侧的墙上）* 下来，夫人，立刻，不然我就上去把你抱下来。*（把帽子搁在书柜上）*

**贝特西**：*（张开双臂，逗引他）* 量你也不敢上来亲我。*（他直朝梯子冲来）*

**安杰莉卡**：*（在梯子右边拦住他）* 慢着，要是有亲可亲，也得从梯子脚下亲起。*（她吻他）* 我把丈夫撇在英格兰，都快想死了。*（又吻他一次）* 早上好，「和气人」。

===Original===
**Betsy**：I’m coming down.

**Hamilton**：I hadn’t the heart to refuse her, Betsy. *（Embraces **Betsy** fondly.）* I’ve been starved so long myself.

**Angelica**：Well, my duties as overseer being finished, I suppose I ought to go, but as I only got a glimpse of you last night, Amiable, I’m going to stay and share you with Betsy for the next five minutes.

**Betsy**：Shall we let her?

**Hamilton**：Yes, yes. Let’s be kind to her.

**Angelica**：Well, the vanity of these men! Now, tell me *all* you’ve been doing since Betsy went away.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：我这就下来。

**汉密尔顿**：我不忍心拒绝她呀，贝特西。*（深情地拥抱贝特西）* 我自己也饿了好久了。

**安杰莉卡**：好了，我这监工的差事算是办完了，照理该走了，可是昨晚我只匆匆看了你一眼，「和气人」，我要再待上五分钟，同贝特西把你分一分。

**贝特西**：咱们让不让她待？

**汉密尔顿**：让，让。咱们对她客气点儿。

**安杰莉卡**：瞧这些男人的臭美劲儿！好了，把贝特西走后你干的*一切*都讲给我听。

===Original===
**Betsy**：You’ve only got five minutes, you know.

**Angelica**：I’ll stay ten.

**Betsy**：Even then I am afraid he will have to leave some things out.

**Angelica**：Ah! Those are the things I should like to hear. Well, what have you been doing?

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：你可只有五分钟，别忘了。

**安杰莉卡**：那我就待十分钟。

**贝特西**：只怕到那时他也得略去一些事情不讲。

**安杰莉卡**：啊！我偏就想听那些。好了，你都干了些什么？

===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（His arm around **Betsy**.）* Well, now I’ll tell you﻿—but pay great attention﻿—these are state secrets. Part of my time has been spent in trying to save this country from rack and ruin.

**Angelica**：What’s rack?

**Betsy**：Order, Mrs. Church.

**Angelica**：I know what ruin is. It’s the salary your country pays you for saving it.

**Hamilton**：Order in court!!

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（手臂搂着贝特西）* 好吧，我这就告诉你——可要仔细听着——这些可都是国家机密。我的一部分时间，花在设法拯救这个国家、免得它彻底倾覆败落上头。

**安杰莉卡**：什么叫倾覆？

**贝特西**：遵守秩序，丘奇太太。

**安杰莉卡**：败落我倒知道。就是你们国家付给你救国的那份薪水。

**汉密尔顿**：本庭肃静！！

===Original===
**Angelica**：If you’re the Judge, you’ve no right to be embracing that lady. *（**Betsy** is nestling during this.）*

**Hamilton**：I’m not the Judge. I’m only the supporting Counsel. But *most* of my time﻿—

**Angelica**：Ah!

**Hamilton**：*（Removing his arm and turning to **Angelica**.）* *Most* of my time has been spent in trying to find the things that Betsy put away before she *went* away.

===Chinese===
**安杰莉卡**：你要是法官，就没权搂着那位女士。*（说话间贝特西正偎在他怀里）*

**汉密尔顿**：我不是法官。我只是辩护律师。不过我的时间*大头*——

**安杰莉卡**：啊！

**汉密尔顿**：*（放下手臂，转向安杰莉卡）* 我的*大头*时间，都花在找东西上了——找贝特西*出远门*之前收起来的那些东西。

===Original===
**Betsy**：*（Turning him toward her.）* Why, Alexander, I put everything in its proper place!

**Hamilton**：That’s what caused all the trouble. That’s why I could never find anything, till I had looked everywhere else.

**Angelica**：Now, if that isn’t just like John Church! Husbands are all alike.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：*（把他的脸扳向自己）* 嗐，亚历山大，我样样东西都放在该放的地方了！

**汉密尔顿**：毛病就出在这上头。所以我样样都找不到，非得把别处都翻遍了才找得着。

**安杰莉卡**：瞧瞧，这不跟约翰·丘奇一个样！天下的丈夫全一个德行。

===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（Looking at **Angelica**, then turning to **Betsy**.）* You’ve seen her husband, Betsy?

**Betsy**：Yes, dear.

**Hamilton**：Has she any right to make that statement?

**Betsy**：No, dear.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（看看安杰莉卡，又转向贝特西）* 你见过她丈夫吧，贝特西？

**贝特西**：见过，亲爱的。

**汉密尔顿**：她有资格下这种断语吗？

**贝特西**：没有，亲爱的。

===Original===
**Hamilton**：Am I like her husband?

**Betsy**：No, dear!

**Hamilton**：Then the court discharges her with a caution. We will proceed with the next case.

**Angelica**：For which, I presume, no witnesses will be called. Well, I’ll go back to my father.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：我像她丈夫吗？

**贝特西**：不像，亲爱的！

**汉密尔顿**：那么，本庭对她予以训诫，当庭开释。我们接着审下一桩案子。

**安杰莉卡**：这一桩嘛，我料想是不用传证人了。好吧，我回父亲那儿去。

===Original===
**Betsy**：Oh, don’t go, Angelica.

**Angelica**：*（Turning C.）* Well, I won’t, because my father’s coming here to fetch me. *（Moves to door L.）* But I’ll go into the kitchen and talk to Mary and Zekiel. *（**Hamilton** makes movement; rises.）*

**Angelica**：No, don’t leave the bench, Judge. I can open the door of my cell. *（Exit L., laughing.）*

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：哦，别走，安杰莉卡。

**安杰莉卡**：*（转身向台中）* 嗐，我不走了，因为父亲一会儿要来这儿接我。*（走向左侧门）* 不过我要到厨房去，同玛丽和泽基尔说说话。*（汉密尔顿作势起身）*

**安杰莉卡**：不，法官大人别离席呀。我自个儿的牢门，我自个儿开得了。*（笑着自左侧下）*

===Original===
**Hamilton**：Betsy!

**Betsy**：*（Fondly.）* You’re a real lover, aren’t you, dear?

**Hamilton**：They’ve been long months without you. It seemed as though you were never coming back.

**Betsy**：It was dreadful of me to stay away so long. But you know I couldn’t help it.

**Hamilton**：You must never, never go away again.

**Betsy**：I never will, dearest. *（She kisses him.）* But now that I am back, I’m going to be a dreadfully expensive wife. I’m going to take away all your savings. There are no end of things wanted for the house﻿—and of course I haven’t got a rag to my back. *（Laughs.）*

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：贝特西！

**贝特西**：*（深情地）* 你真是个多情的情郎，是不是，亲爱的？

**汉密尔顿**：没有你的这些日子，可真漫长。你仿佛永远不回来了似的。

**贝特西**：我离开这么久，实在不像话。可你知道，我实在没法子。

**汉密尔顿**：你再也不许、再也不许走了。

**贝特西**：再也不走了，最亲爱的。*（她吻他）* 可我这一回来，就要做个花钱吓死人的妻子了。我要把你的积蓄统统花光。家里要添的东西没完没了——当然喽，我身上连一块像样的布片都没有。*（笑）*

===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（Laughing uneasily.）* Well, Betsy, we’ll pay a visit to the rag shop first, and the house can wait.

**Betsy**：No, no, we’ll do the house first﻿—and I’ll wait. But you must have untold wealth hidden away. Why, there’s two quarters’ salary since I’ve been gone﻿—and no wife to spend it for you.

**Hamilton**：*（Distressed.）* Betsy, I’ve had some unexpected expenses recently﻿—but *next* quarter﻿—

**Betsy**：Why, it’s all right, dear. Everything can wait. Only I didn’t know. I thought you’d been at home, busy, and hadn’t much opportunity of spending.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（笑得不大自在）* 好吧，贝特西，咱们先上「布片店」走一遭，家里的事往后放放。

**贝特西**：不，不，先办家里的事——我等着。你手头一定攒下了一笔没数的家财。嗐，我走后你足足攒了两个季度的薪水呢——又没有太太替你花。

**汉密尔顿**：*（局促不安）* 贝特西，我近来添了些意外的开销——不过*下个*季度——

**贝特西**：嗐，没关系，亲爱的。什么都可以等。我不过是先前不知道罢了。我还当你一直在家，忙着公事，没什么花钱的机会呢。

===Original===
**Hamilton**：I’ve had some expensive presents to make﻿—diplomatic presents, you know.

**Betsy**：*（With a little laugh.）* Oh, bribes.

**Hamilton**：*（Glancing at her quickly.）* I’m afraid it almost amounts to that.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：我送出去几份贵重的礼——外交礼物，你知道的。

**贝特西**：*（轻笑一声）* 哦，贿赂。

**汉密尔顿**：*（飞快地瞥她一眼）* 恐怕差不多就是这个意思了。

===Original===
**Betsy**：That doesn’t sound like you, Alexander. I can’t bear to think﻿—*（Knock heard. They rise.）* Now, here’s somebody coming to take you away from me, I suppose.

**Hamilton**：*（Embracing and kissing her.）* This is your day, Betsy, and wild horses shall not drag me from you.

**Schuyler**：*（Off stage.）* Wait a moment, Judge. I’ll see if he’s at home. Mr. Hamilton at home, Uncle?

**Zekiel**：*（Off.）* Walk right in, sah.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：这可不像你说的话，亚历山大。我不忍往下想——*（传来敲门声。两人起身）* 得，我猜又有人来把你从我身边抢走了。

**汉密尔顿**：*（拥抱她，吻她）* 今天是你的日子，贝特西，就是套上野马也休想把我从你身边拽走。

**斯凯勒**：*（幕后）* 您稍等，大法官。我去瞧瞧他在不在家。老伯，汉密尔顿先生在家吗？

**泽基尔**：*（幕后）* 您里边请，先生。

===Original===
**Schuyler**：*（Coming to C. behind **Betsy**.）* Here’s Chief Justice Jay, Alexander. I met him on the doorstep.

**Betsy**：*（Up stage C., curtseying.）* How do you do, Judge Jay?

**Jay**：Mrs. Hamilton *（bowing near door up R. and coming down）*, I’m delighted to welcome you back.

**Schuyler**：*（To **Betsy**.）* Good morning, my dear.

**Betsy**：Good morning, father.

===Chinese===
**斯凯勒**：*（走到贝特西身后的台中）* 亚历山大，首席大法官杰伊来了。我在门阶上撞见的。

**贝特西**：*（行至台中后方，行屈膝礼）* 您好，杰伊大法官？

**杰伊**：汉密尔顿夫人*（在右上方门边躬身行礼，随即走下）*，欢迎您回来，我高兴极了。

**斯凯勒**：*（对贝特西）* 早上好，我亲爱的。

**贝特西**：早上好，父亲。
===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（Giving his hand.）* Judge Jay, it is good of you to honor us with a visit. *（**Jay** comes to **Hamilton**.）* You’ve been away.

**Jay**：*（Down R. in front of sofa.）* Yes, for a month. On my return I went first to General Washington, whom I now find installed in Colonel Frank’s house at Germantown.

**Hamilton**：Yes, the place was available as temporary executive quarters.

**Jay**：I then came to pay my respects to Mrs. Hamilton and to congratulate you on your having converted Jefferson and Monroe to the support of your Bill.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（伸出手去）* 杰伊大法官，承蒙您屈尊来访，实在客气。*（杰伊走到汉密尔顿跟前）* 您出了一趟远门。

**杰伊**：*（走到沙发前右侧）* 是，去了一个月。回来后我先去拜见华盛顿将军，如今他已住进杰曼敦弗兰克上校的宅子了。

**汉密尔顿**：是的，那宅子正好可用作临时的行政官邸。

**杰伊**：随后我就来向汉密尔顿夫人致敬，并恭喜您，居然把杰斐逊和门罗争取过来支持您的州债法案了。

===Original===
**Hamilton**：That is gradually filtering through to the other states, eh?

**Schuyler**：Alexander’s a wonder!

**Hamilton**：You know, Jefferson and Monroe have withheld the news of their capitulation as long as possible. They’re desperately afraid of what their followers will say.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：这消息正一点一点传到别的州去，嗯？

**斯凯勒**：亚历山大真是个奇才！

**汉密尔顿**：你知道，杰斐逊和门罗把他们认输的消息捂得越久越好。他们生怕自己的追随者会说闲话。

===Original===
**Jay**：It’s amazing. I didn’t believe that Jefferson would yield one inch.

**Schuyler**：After all his yelling and shouting about the Rights of Man! Alexander’s a wonder!

**Jay**：*（Laughing.）* What excuse will he make to his constituents?

**Hamilton**：*（C. **Schuyler** on his L., **Jay** on his R., **Betsy** on his L.）* He’ll just take his pen in his hand and write a cantata and his constituents will lift up their voices and sing. *（All laugh.）*

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：真是想不到。我原先不信杰斐逊肯退让一寸。

**斯凯勒**：他可是把人权嚷嚷得震天响的人！亚历山大真是个奇才！

**杰伊**：*（笑）* 他打算怎么向他的选民交代？

**汉密尔顿**：*（居中。斯凯勒在他左侧，杰伊在他右侧，贝特西在他左边）* 他只需提笔写上一首康塔塔，他的选民们便会齐声高歌。*（众人大笑）*

===Original===
**Schuyler**：Sing what?

**Hamilton**：Jefferson’s praises, of course.

===Chinese===
**斯凯勒**：唱什么？

**汉密尔顿**：当然是颂扬杰斐逊的歌。

===Original===
**Betsy**：How did you do it?

**Schuyler**：Bribery. Bribery and corruption!

**Betsy**：You didn’t give Mr. Jefferson your two quarters’ salary?

**Hamilton**：No, my dear. I gave him the Capital of the United States.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：你是怎么做到的？

**斯凯勒**：行贿。行贿加舞弊！

**贝特西**：你不会是把两个季度的薪水送给杰斐逊先生了吧？

**汉密尔顿**：没有，亲爱的。我送给他的是合众国的首都。

===Original===
**Betsy**：But there isn’t one!

**Hamilton**：That’s what made it so easy.

**Schuyler**：Ah, if it could only have been in Albany!

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：可首都还没有呢！

**汉密尔顿**：所以才好送啊。

**斯凯勒**：唉，要是能定在奥尔巴尼就好了！

===Original===
**Jay**：*（With a touch of indignation.）* Albany? Why put the Capital at the other end of the river? It should be in New York.

**Hamilton**：*（Coming down between them, laughing.）* Let us resign it to Jefferson﻿—on the Potomac. It’s a long way from civilization, and the river will carry off the refuse of debate. The representative of the separate states can pour their slander and vituperation into the Potomac﻿—until they damn it, while the Hudson will remain unrestrained to carry on the business of the country.

**Betsy**：*（Curtseying to **Jay**.）* Good morning, Your Honor. I must tell you that my husband has promised this day to me.

**Hamilton**：Are you on business, Judge Jay?

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：*（带一点愤愤然）* 奥尔巴尼？干吗把首都放到河的那一头？就该定在纽约。

**汉密尔顿**：*（笑着走到两人中间）* 咱们就把它让给杰斐逊吧——放在波托马克河上。那儿离文明十万八千里，河水正好把辩论的污物冲走。各州的代表们尽可以把他们的诽谤咒骂统统倒进波托马克河——倒到把河都堵死为止，哈得孙河却依旧畅流无阻，好办国家正事。

**贝特西**：*（向杰伊行屈膝礼）* 祝您早安，大人。我得告诉您，我丈夫可是把今天许给我了。

**汉密尔顿**：您是有公事吗，杰伊大法官？

===Original===
**Jay**：*（Down R.）* I think I shall not detain you long.

**Hamilton**：Then, my dear﻿—*（Coming to her by R. of table.）*

**Betsy**：*（Smiling.）* You said wild horses should not drag you from me.

**Hamilton**：I think the Judge hardly comes under that class; you see he is controlled by the harness of the state.

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：*（走到右前方）* 我想不会耽搁你们太久。

**汉密尔顿**：那么，亲爱的——*（走到桌右她身边）*

**贝特西**：*（微笑）* 你可是说过，套上野马也休想把你从我身边拽走。

**汉密尔顿**：我想大法官够不上那一类；你瞧，他可是套着国家挽具的呢。

===Original===
**Jay**：*（Laughing and to C.）* Which makes him a very tame horse indeed.

**Betsy**：In ten minutes, then, I shall take the reins and drive you away.

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：*（笑着走到台中）* 那他可真成了一匹驯顺无比的马了。

**贝特西**：那么，十分钟后，我就来接过缰绳，把你赶回家去。

===Original===
**Jay**：You must be proud of your daughters, General Schuyler.

**Schuyler**：Proud? Why, since they’ve been back my bosom has so swelled with pride that my foot has sunk into insignificance.

**Hamilton**：*（Taking chair from L. of table and placing it L. C. in front of table.）* Will you sit down, Judge? *（Indicates chair R. of table.）* There was something you wished to say to me?

**Jay**：*（Coming to chair R. of table.）* Yes﻿—er﻿—yes. Ahem! What a very pleasant room you have here.

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：斯凯勒将军，您一定为您的女儿们自豪吧。

**斯凯勒**：自豪？嗐，自打她们回来，我胸口那份得意涨得满满的，连我那只痛风脚都显得不值一提了。

**汉密尔顿**：*（从桌子左边搬过一把椅子，放在桌前中左）* 大法官，您请坐。*（示意桌右的椅子）* 您有话要同我说吧？

**杰伊**：*（走到桌右的椅子旁）* 是——呃——是。啊哼！您这间屋子真是赏心悦目。

===Original===
**Hamilton**：I’m beginning to doubt it, Judge Jay.

**Jay**：*（Surprised.）* To doubt it? Why?

**Hamilton**：That is precisely the remark Mr. Jefferson made when he had something unpleasant to say to me.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：我倒开始犯嘀咕了，杰伊大法官。

**杰伊**：*（讶然）* 犯嘀咕？为什么？

**汉密尔顿**：杰斐逊先生有难言之隐要同我讲的时候，说的恰恰就是这句话。

===Original===
**Jay**：You are right. I have something that it is not easy to say to you.

**Schuyler**：Do you wish me to go, Judge?

**Jay**：No, General, I very much desire that you should stay. Mr. Hamilton, it is sometimes difficult to determine how far a public man realizes the extent of his influence on the character of others. You are a modest man, but I think you must know that the eyes of the nation are turned toward you as an honorable man who is to steer this country clear of grave dangers.

**Hamilton**：I am, at any rate, conscious of great responsibilities.

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：您说得对。我确实有一件难以启齿的事要告诉您。

**斯凯勒**：您要我回避吗，大法官？

**杰伊**：不，将军，我非常希望您留下。汉密尔顿先生，一个公众人物对自己影响他人品行的程度究竟意识到几分，有时实在难以断定。您是个谦逊的人，但我想您必定知道：举国上下都在注视着您，把您看作一位将引领这个国家避开深重危难的正直之士。

**汉密尔顿**：无论如何，我深知自己责任重大。

===Original===
**Jay**：That is so﻿—great and grave responsibilities. No one but yourself can persuade the government to assume the debts of the states.

**Hamilton**：The value of the cooperation of Jefferson and Monroe on that issue cannot be overrated.

**Jay**：Yes. The Anti-Federalists are with you on that issue as long as they have to be, but it is my belief that they are looking for a loophole, a way out.

**Hamilton**：What makes you believe that?

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：正是——重大而严峻的责任。除您之外，无人能说服政府承担各州的债务。

**汉密尔顿**：在这件事上，杰斐逊和门罗的合作，其价值怎么高估都不为过。

**杰伊**：是啊。反联邦党人在这个问题上是不得不站在您一边，但我相信他们正在找退路，找一条脱身的缝儿。

**汉密尔顿**：您凭什么这样想？

===Original===
**Jay**：*（Pause. **Hamilton** waits.）* I will be frank with you. I have been several times annoyed by the receipt of anonymous letters threatening exposure of some scandal connected with the Secretary of the Treasury. I wished you to remain, General Schuyler, because I want it understood that my confidence in Colonel Hamilton is unbounded. I have traced these letters to a man named Reynolds.

**Hamilton**：Oh, yes.

**Jay**：You know him?

**Hamilton**：Yes.

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：*（停顿。汉密尔顿等待着）* 我对您直说吧。我几次三番收到匿名信，威胁要揭发一桩牵涉财政部长的丑闻，深为所扰。斯凯勒将军，我所以要您留下，是因为我想让人明白：我对汉密尔顿上校的信任是毫无保留的。这些信，我已经追查到一个姓雷诺兹的人头上。

**汉密尔顿**：哦，是他。

**杰伊**：您认识他？

**汉密尔顿**：认识。

===Original===
**Jay**：He was recently arrested for perjury in connection with a state case. Do you know anything of the case?

**Hamilton**：No, but the prosecution was made through the Treasury Department, and he wrote to me, begging me to use my influence to obtain his release.

**Jay**：And you refused.

**Hamilton**：Yes, I refused.

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：他最近因一桩州里案件的伪证罪被捕了。您了解这桩案子吗？

**汉密尔顿**：不了解，只是那场起诉是经财政部提起的，他写信给我，求我施加影响放他出来。

**杰伊**：您拒绝了。

**汉密尔顿**：是的，我拒绝了。

===Original===
**Jay**：Had he any reason to believe that you would help him?

**Hamilton**：Yes, good reason.

**Jay**：Why did you refuse?

**Hamilton**：Because I have never used my public office for my private ends.

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：他有什么理由相信您会帮他吗？

**汉密尔顿**：有，很充足的理由。

**杰伊**：那您为什么拒绝？

**汉密尔顿**：因为我从不曾利用公职谋一己之私。

===Original===
**Jay**：By that I understand that the man has some claim upon you.

**Hamilton**：Yes.

**Jay**：It has come to my knowledge that since his arrest certain papers or letters have fallen into the hands of Senator Monroe, and that some information has been passed on to the unscrupulous Giles of Virginia. *（Enter **Zekiel** up R.）*

**Zekiel**：Sentah Monroe an’ Mr. Giles to see yo’, sah.

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：这么一说，我明白，那人手心里是攥着您什么的。

**汉密尔顿**：是的。

**杰伊**：我还得知，自他被捕之后，若干文件和信件落到了门罗参议员手里，一些内情也传到了弗吉尼亚那个不讲道义的贾尔斯耳中。*（泽基尔自右上登场）*

**泽基尔**：门罗参议员跟贾尔斯先生求见，先生。

===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（Rising.）* It looks as if your information were correct, Judge Jay.

**Jay**：*（Rising.）* I’ll take my leave.

**Hamilton**：Excuse me. I ask you both to remain. Show them in, Zekiel.

**Jay**：*（Meeting him in front of table and taking his hand.）* Understand, Hamilton, my confidence in your integrity is unshakable.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（起身）* 看来您的消息不假，杰伊大法官。

**杰伊**：*（起身）* 我这就告辞。

**汉密尔顿**：慢来。我请您二位都留下。泽基尔，请他们进来。

**杰伊**：*（在桌前迎住他，握住他的手）* 你要明白，汉密尔顿，我对你的操守的信心是不可动摇的。

===Original===
**Zekiel**：Sentah Monroe and Mr. Giles.

**Hamilton**：*（Down C. and bowing.）* Gentlemen, you honor me. *（Exit **Zekiel** door up R.）*

**Monroe**：*（Coming down.）* I’m sorry if I interrupt you, Mr. Hamilton, but my errand is important.

===Chinese===
**泽基尔**：门罗参议员，贾尔斯先生到。

**汉密尔顿**：*（走到台中，躬身）* 二位先生，不胜荣幸。*（泽基尔自右上门下）*

**门罗**：*（走下前来）* 汉密尔顿先生，若有打扰，还望恕罪，但我此来有要事。

===Original===
**Giles**：*（On **Monroe’s** R.）* How do, Hamilton?

**Hamilton**：Mr. Giles. *（Bows.）*

**Monroe**：Citizen Jay.

**Jay**：Senator Monroe. *（They bow.）*

**Giles**：How do, Judge? *（**Jay** doesn’t bow to **Giles**.）*

===Chinese===
**贾尔斯**：*（在门罗右侧）* 你好啊，汉密尔顿？

**汉密尔顿**：贾尔斯先生。*（躬身）*

**门罗**：杰伊公民。

**杰伊**：门罗参议员。*（两人互相躬身）*

**贾尔斯**：你好啊，大法官？*（杰伊不向贾尔斯还礼）*

===Original===
**Hamilton**：Judge Jay called﻿—Won’t you sit down, Mr. Monroe? *（**Monroe** sits R. of table. **Giles** remains standing.）* Judge Jay called to express his pleasure at your cooperation in connection with the States’ Debts. *（**Jay** sits L. of table.）*

**Monroe**：Yes, I trust we shall have no reason to alter our decision on that point.

**Hamilton**：*（Standing back of table.）* I, too, trust that you will not find it expedient to break your word.

**Giles**：It’s not a matter of breaking words; it’s a matter of public opinion.

**Hamilton**：Oh, I was not referring to you, Mr. Giles. Everybody knows that you would never be guilty of a breach of faith.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：杰伊大法官方才还——门罗先生，您不坐吗？*（门罗在桌右落座。贾尔斯仍旧站着）* 杰伊大法官此来，正是为你们在州债一事上的合作表示欣慰。*（杰伊在桌左落座）*

**门罗**：是的，但愿我们不会有什么理由在那件事上改变决定。

**汉密尔顿**：*（站到桌后）* 我也希望，你们不至于觉得食言对自己更有利。

**贾尔斯**：这不是食不食言的事，是民意的事。

**汉密尔顿**：哦，我说的不是您，贾尔斯先生。谁都知道，您是决计不会背信弃义的。

===Original===
**Giles**：Ugh! *（Moves across R. in front of sofa.）*

**Hamilton**：Unless it were made absolutely worth your while.

**Giles**：*（Turning.）* That remark is uncalled for, sir!

**Hamilton**：True. Tell me, Mr. Giles, what *have* you called for?

===Chinese===
**贾尔斯**：哼！*（横穿到沙发前的右侧）*

**汉密尔顿**：——除非好处实在是够大。

**贾尔斯**：*（转过身）* 先生，这话可是平白无故的冒犯！

**汉密尔顿**：不错。那么告诉我，贾尔斯先生，你们*到底*所为何来？

===Original===
**Monroe**：We have called, Citizen Hamilton, on business of a private character.

**Hamilton**：Do you wish us to be alone?

**Monroe**：I think you would prefer it.

**Zekiel**：*（Enters up R., announcing.）* Mr. Thomas Jefferson!

===Chinese===
**门罗**：公民汉密尔顿，我们来，是为一件私事。

**汉密尔顿**：你们要同我单独谈吗？

**门罗**：我想您会更希望如此。

**泽基尔**：*（自右上进来，通报）* 托马斯·杰斐逊先生到！

===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（Greets him C.）* Mr. Jefferson!

**Jefferson**：You will excuse me, Citizen Hamilton, but I received a note from Citizen Monroe asking me to meet him here at this hour. I trust I do not intrude.

**Monroe**：I told Mr. Jefferson that the business was urgent.

**Jefferson**：Otherwise, I assure you, I should not have taken this liberty.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（在台中迎住他）* 杰斐逊先生！

**杰斐逊**：还望见谅，公民汉密尔顿，我收到公民门罗的条子，约我此时在此相会。但愿没有打扰。

**门罗**：我告诉杰斐逊先生，事情十分紧急。

**杰斐逊**：若非如此，我向您保证，我决不会这样贸然造次。
===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（To **Monroe**.）* Does your business relate to my public office as Secretary of the Treasury?

**Giles**：It does.

**Hamilton**：Then I am gratified that you are here, Mr. Jefferson, and I shall ask you, gentlemen, to allow Chief Justice Jay and General Schuyler to remain.

**Monroe**：*（Stiffly.）* If you wish it.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（对门罗）* 你们的事，与我财政部长这公职有关吗？

**贾尔斯**：有关。

**汉密尔顿**：那么，杰斐逊先生，您在此我倒高兴了。诸位先生，请容我请首席大法官杰伊和斯凯勒将军留下。

**门罗**：*（生硬地）* 随您的便。

===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（Taking chair from behind sofa and placing it C. **Jefferson** shakes hands with **Schuyler** and then sits.）* Pray be seated, Mr. Jefferson.

**Monroe**：Mr. Hamilton, I am compelled to ask you if you are acquainted﻿—

**Hamilton**：It may facilitate matters if I say that I *am* acquainted with a man of the name of Reynolds.

**Giles**：*（Standing down R. in front of sofa.）* James Reynolds.

**Hamilton**：I’m not sure of his first name. He is hardly an intimate acquaintance of mine. I believe it is James. You probably know him as Jim. Won’t you sit down? *（**Giles** does not hear him. **Hamilton** repeats.）* Won’t you sit down?

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（从沙发后取过一把椅子放在台中。杰斐逊与斯凯勒握手后坐下）* 请坐，杰斐逊先生。

**门罗**：汉密尔顿先生，我不得不问您，您是否结识——

**汉密尔顿**：我先把话说明白，或许事情好办些——我*确实*结识一个姓雷诺兹的人。

**贾尔斯**：*（站在沙发前右下方）* 詹姆斯·雷诺兹。

**汉密尔顿**：他的名字我记不准了。他算不上我的至交。想必是叫詹姆斯。你们大概管他叫吉姆。您不坐吗？*（贾尔斯没听见。汉密尔顿又说一遍）* 您不坐吗？

===Original===
**Monroe**：The man Reynolds has placed in my hands certain letters which show that he has recently received from you considerable sums of money.

**Hamilton**：You are apparently in his confidence, Mr. Monroe.

**Giles**：Those letters were brought to us unasked.

**Hamilton**：I see. The letters were also uncalled for.

===Chinese===
**门罗**：那个雷诺兹把几封信交到我手里，信上表明，他近来从您手里收到过数目可观的款项。

**汉密尔顿**：看来您是他的心腹了，门罗先生。

**贾尔斯**：那些信是不请自来，送到我们手上的。

**汉密尔顿**：原来如此。这些信也是「平白无故」送上门的。

===Original===
**Monroe**：You say you are not intimately acquainted﻿—what was that money paid for?

**Hamilton**：May I be allowed to see the documents in question?

**Giles**：*（After **Hamilton** has looked at a few of them.）* What was that money paid for?

**Hamilton**：Didn’t your friend tell you﻿—I mean before he was arrested for perjury?

===Chinese===
**门罗**：您说你们并非深交——那笔钱是做什么用的？

**汉密尔顿**：能否容我看一看有关的信件？

**贾尔斯**：*（汉密尔顿看过几封之后）* 那笔钱是做什么用的？

**汉密尔顿**：您的朋友没告诉您吗——我是说，在他因伪证罪被捕之前？
===Original===
**Giles**：*（Hotly.）* He’s not my friend! I never saw the man till he came to me on the subject of these letters.

**Hamilton**：Are you sure?

**Giles**：Of course I’m sure!

===Chinese===
**贾尔斯**：*（火气上来）* 他不是我的朋友！在这些信之前，我从没见过这个人。

**汉密尔顿**：您肯定？

**贾尔斯**：当然肯定！
===Original===
**Hamilton**：That is George Washington’s chair!

**Giles**：*（Angrily pulls chair around to face **Hamilton**.）* There seems to be no doubt that you paid him the money.

**Hamilton**：There seems to be no doubt about that.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：那是乔治·华盛顿的椅子！

**贾尔斯**：*（气冲冲地把椅子转过来对着汉密尔顿）* 您付给他钱，这一点看来是无疑的了。

**汉密尔顿**：这一点，看来确实是无疑的。
===Original===
**Monroe**：Mr. Hamilton, I should not be here if I had not been forced by the facts before me. But I cannot disbelieve the evidence of my own eyes. There are your letters proving that the money was paid to Reynolds, and we are compelled to ask you why you paid it.

**Hamilton**：Who compels you to do that?

**Monroe**：Our sense of duty, Citizen Hamilton.

**Hamilton**：Duty to whom?

===Chinese===
**门罗**：汉密尔顿先生，若非眼前的事实所迫，我不会坐在这里。可我不能不信自己亲眼所见。这儿是您的信，证明钱付给了雷诺兹，我们不得不问您，这钱是为什么付的。

**汉密尔顿**：谁逼你们问的？

**门罗**：是我们的责任感，公民汉密尔顿。

**汉密尔顿**：对谁的责任？
===Original===
**Monroe**：To the country﻿—to the people﻿—to the citizens of this Republic.

**Hamilton**：This has nothing to do with the country or the people. This is my own private affair.

**Giles**：We know why you paid the money﻿—Reynolds told us.

**Hamilton**：Oh, you know, do you?

===Chinese===
**门罗**：对国家——对人民——对共和国的公民。

**汉密尔顿**：这件事同国家、同人民毫无干系。这是我个人的私事。

**贾尔斯**：我们知道你为什么付钱——雷诺兹告诉我们了。

**汉密尔顿**：哦，你们知道了，是吗？
===Original===
**Giles**：Yes, we know.

**Hamilton**：Then you haven’t come for information﻿—but merely for the love of sport.

**Monroe**：That money was paid out of the Treasury of the United States.

**Hamilton**：It was paid out of my own personal account.

===Chinese===
**贾尔斯**：对，我们知道。

**汉密尔顿**：那你们就不是来打听消息的——不过是图个打猎的兴致罢了。

**门罗**：那笔钱是从合众国财政部里支出来的。

**汉密尔顿**：那钱是从我个人的账户里支出来的。
===Original===
**Monroe**：That we shall require to have proved. But you know what it was paid for.

**Hamilton**：Yes, I know what it was paid for, and from what Mr. Giles says, you know also.

**Monroe**：I do. It was paid to this man that he might buy up the states’ paper﻿—the states’ debts.

**Hamilton**：*（Stopping opening drawer quickly.）* What do you mean?

===Chinese===
**门罗**：这一点我们要你们拿出证明。但你知道那钱是做什么用的。

**汉密尔顿**：是的，我知道那钱是做什么用的，而且听贾尔斯先生的口气，你们也知道。

**门罗**：我们知道。付钱给这个人，是要他收购各州证券——各州的债务。

**汉密尔顿**：*（猛地停住正要开抽屉的手）* 你这是什么意思？
===Original===
**Monroe**：That he might buy up the states’ debts, for your benefit.

**Hamilton**：*（Rising.）* What!

**Monroe**：A stock-jobbing gamble.

**Jay**：Come, come, Mr. Monroe. You have only the man’s word for that.

===Chinese===
**门罗**：要他收购各州债务——为你谋利。

**汉密尔顿**：*（起身）* 什么！

**门罗**：一场证券投机的赌博。

**杰伊**：得了，得了，门罗先生。这话不过是那人的片面之词。
===Original===
**Schuyler**：The word of a man who is arrested for perjury.

**Monroe**：*（Turning to **Schuyler**.）* Reynolds gives conclusive evidence that Mr. Hamilton gave him advance information of the proposed Government Assumption of States’ Debts; that he gave him the money to buy up the paper at bargain prices; and that his share in the spoils will be five million dollars.

**Hamilton**：*（Genuinely surprised.）* So that’s what he told you, did he? That’s the reason for the whole﻿—thing.

**Monroe**：And there are the proofs of the money you gave him.

===Chinese===
**斯凯勒**：一个因伪证罪被捕的人的片面之词。

**门罗**：*（转向斯凯勒）* 雷诺兹给出了确凿的证据：汉密尔顿先生把政府拟承担各州债务的机密事先透露给他；给他钱让他贱价收购那些证券；而他从赃利中分得的一份，将是五百万美元。

**汉密尔顿**：*（当真吃了一惊）* 原来他是这么跟你们说的，是吗？原来整桩——事情的根由在这儿。

**门罗**：这儿还有您给他钱的凭证。
===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（After a pause and looking through letters.）* Thirty dollars﻿—twenty-five dollars﻿—one hundred dollars﻿—Very modest amounts for purposes of speculation.

**Jay**：Very.

**Giles**：Just as dishonest to steal ten dollars as ten millions.

**Monroe**：You can’t dodge, Hamilton. I owe it to this country to expose this damned business. You use your official information to rob the patriots who raised the money to save this nation!

**Jay**：No! No!

**Schuyler**：Mr. Monroe!

**Monroe**：He makes them believe their loans will never be paid, and then he hires this man to buy up their claims﻿—and hoodwinks us into passing his bill for him.

**Jay**：Senator Monroe, we entirely lack proof of this.

**Hamilton**：Gentlemen﻿—let us try to be frank with one another. You have come here today not because you believe me guilty of this accusation, not because you feel any duty to the public, but because you repent the bargain you made with me to vote for my bill. You are afraid of your own party. Your courage has failed you, and you believe this to be a tremendous opportunity to free yourselves from your promise. *（**Jefferson** protests.）* That is the true statement of fact, Mr. Jefferson, whatever you may think to the contrary. You dislike me﻿—you are afraid of me﻿—and this is part of an organized conspiracy to force me to resign, and so to end your difficulties. This is not the first time that you have accused me, but it is the first time that I have been unwilling to strike back at you. Time and again you have charged me with dishonesty in the Treasury. Three months ago you set the trusty Giles on to covertly accuse me of cooking the accounts. Within ten days, as you know, after going through endless records, I proved that there was no shadow of foundation for your accusation, and you slunk away whipped and defeated. And now here you are yelping at my heels again and ready to tear me to pieces. This time you are bolder. You come to me with an open accusation of absolute dishonesty. This accusation is based on the evidence of one James Reynolds, an obscure and worthless man. Had I desired to defraud the Treasury should I have been driven to the necessity of unkennelling Reynolds to assist me? Gentlemen, it is not a reasonable accusation. If I felt that you really believed that this was even remotely connected with my office, I should not hesitate to give you proof to the contrary. But you know that what you accuse me of has no foundation in truth. *（Pause.）* I admit that I had transactions with the man Reynolds *（movement of interest from the others）*, but they were of a private nature, and I swear that my connection with him is in no way bound up with my public office. I make an appeal to your sense of justice, and I ask you, gentlemen, to drop this investigation.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（顿了顿，翻看信件）* 三十美元——二十五美元——一百美元——拿来投机，数目未免太寒酸了些。

**杰伊**：确实寒酸。

**贾尔斯**：偷十美元跟偷一千万，一样是不干净。

**门罗**：你逃不掉的，汉密尔顿。为了这个国家，我有责任揭穿这桩该死的勾当。你利用官家的消息，去掠夺那些出钱救了这国家的爱国志士！

**杰伊**：不对！不对！

**斯凯勒**：门罗先生！

**门罗**：他叫他们相信借出去的债永无偿还之日，然后雇下这个人，把他们的债权贱价收买——再连哄带骗，让我们替他通过他的州债法案。

**杰伊**：门罗参议员，这一切我们全无实据。

**汉密尔顿**：诸位——我们不妨彼此坦率些。你们今日到这儿来，并不是因为相信我罪名属实，也不是出于对公众的责任感，而是因为你们懊悔了同我做的那笔交易——投票支持我的州债法案。你们怕自己的党。你们的胆气泄了，只当这是一个天大的机会，好从你们的承诺里脱身。*（杰斐逊表示抗议）* 杰斐逊先生，任凭您心里怎么不以为然，这才是事实的真相。你们不喜欢我——你们怕我——而这是那场有组织的阴谋的一部分，要逼我辞职，好就此了结你们的难处。你们攻讦我，这不是头一回；但我不愿还手，这却是头一回。你们一次又一次指控我在财政部不清不白。三个月前，你们放出这条忠心的走狗贾尔斯，暗中诬我做假账。不出十天，你们是知道的，我翻遍了无穷无尽的账册卷宗，证明你们的指控连一丝一毫的影子都没有，你们便夹着尾巴、灰溜溜地败下阵去。如今你们又追着我的脚后跟狂吠，恨不得把我撕成碎片。这一回你们胆子大了。你们带着一桩公开的、彻头彻尾的不老实罪名找上门来。这指控所依据的，不过是一个詹姆斯·雷诺兹的证词——一个无足轻重的卑贱小人。倘若我真想侵吞财政部，难道还会非得把雷诺兹这么个货色从耗子洞里掏出来帮我不可？诸位，这指控不合情理。倘若我觉得你们当真相信此事同我的公职哪怕有一星半点的牵连，我会毫不犹豫地拿出反证来。可你们自己清楚，你们指控我的事，在事实上毫无根据。*（停顿）* 我承认，我同雷诺兹这个人有过银钱往来*（众人闻之动容）*，但那纯属私事，我发誓，我同他的瓜葛同我的公职绝无半点纠葛。我诉诸诸位的正义感，并请求诸位先生，就此罢手，不要再查了。
===Original===
**Jay**：*（After a slight pause.）* In deference to Mr. Hamilton, I think, gentlemen, that should close the matter.

**Schuyler**：There is no question of the honor of Alexander Hamilton.

**Jefferson**：Mr. Hamilton has indeed made a very moving appeal.

**Monroe**：If the facts are not as stated, why not tell us what they are?

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：*（略停片刻）* 看在汉密尔顿先生面上，诸位，我想这件事就此了结了吧。

**斯凯勒**：亚历山大·汉密尔顿的名誉，不容置疑。

**杰斐逊**：汉密尔顿先生这一番陈情，确实动人。

**门罗**：倘若事实并非如此，为什么不说出来呢？

===Original===
**Hamilton**：Because such disclosure would be useless to you and would cause much suffering to some who are very near and dear to me.

**Jefferson**：*（As if about to rise.）* Well, in that case, Citizen Hamilton﻿—

**Monroe**：But I don’t see how﻿—Do you mean it’s a domestic affair?

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：因为和盘托出对你们毫无用处，却会给我身边至亲至爱的人带来莫大的痛苦。

**杰斐逊**：*（作势欲起）* 那么，既然如此，公民汉密尔顿——

**门罗**：可我不明白这——您是说，这是家事？

===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（Uneasily.）* In a sense, yes.

**Monroe**：Something that affects your wife?

**Hamilton**：*（Restraining himself.）* Yes, sir. Something that affects my wife﻿—

**Giles**：*（Rising.）* Mr. Hamilton, as a trusted representative of the State of Virginia, I feel it my duty to ask you a few questions.

**Hamilton**：*（Looks at **Giles** hard for several moments and then sits. Great restraint.）* Yes.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（局促地）* 从某种意义上说，是的。

**门罗**：是牵涉到您太太的事？

**汉密尔顿**：*（强自克制）* 是的，先生。是牵涉到我太太的事——

**贾尔斯**：*（起身）* 汉密尔顿先生，作为弗吉尼亚州受信赖的代表，我觉得有责任问您几个问题。

**汉密尔顿**：*（狠狠盯了贾尔斯半晌，随后坐下。极力克制）* 问吧。

===Original===
**Giles**：In the first place, in what way is your wife mixed up﻿—

**Hamilton**：*（Springing up and striking table. Furious.）* Stop! There shall be no more of this. I am ashamed of the act that has led to this inquiry, but I am more ashamed of my cowardice in begging your charity. You shall have the facts. During the absence of my wife abroad, I became intimate with Mrs. Reynolds﻿—*（Sensation.）* It doesn’t matter how or where, but the thing happened﻿—to my eternal disgrace. Since that night I have been paying hush-money to the man Reynolds. He has never ceased to blackmail me. *（Taking letters from drawer.）* Here are his letters with demands for money. They form the sequel to those which you now hold; and here are three or four love letters from Mrs. Reynolds, which I am sure you will enjoy reading.

===Chinese===
**贾尔斯**：首先，您太太是怎么牵扯进去的——

**汉密尔顿**：*（霍然跃起，一拳砸在桌上。暴怒）* 住口！不能再这样下去了。我对引发这场盘问的行径感到羞耻，可我更羞耻的，是自己方才竟怯懦地乞求你们的宽贷。你们会得到事实的。在我妻子旅居国外期间，我同雷诺兹夫人有了私情——*（满座哗然）* 是怎么开始、在什么地方，都无关紧要，反正事情发生了——成了我终身洗不掉的耻辱。自那一夜起，我就一直在给雷诺兹这个人付封口钱。他对我的敲诈，从来没有停过。*（从抽屉里取出一叠信）* 这些是他的信，一封封都是要钱的，正是你们手里那些信的后续；这几封是雷诺兹夫人写来的情书，三四封呢，我保证诸位读来津津有味。

===Original===
**Schuyler**：*（Rising. Thunderstruck.）* Alexander!

**Hamilton**：Father, I have no excuse to make. *（**Giles** picks up the letters and crosses quickly and shows them to **Jefferson**. **Jefferson** turns away saying “No, no.” **Giles** then turns to **Monroe**. **Monroe** takes the letters and throws them on the table saying: “I prefer not.” **Hamilton** comes back of table.）* Since this accusation has been made I must insist on your reading all these documents. These are his﻿—and these are hers. They both form part of the plot in which you are now involved. You will find no mention of state debts in them. Her letters are an attempt to drag me into a prolonged intrigue, and were necessary for the full effect of his blackmailing. *（Looking at **Giles** who is standing C. between **Monroe** and **Jefferson**.）* A conspiracy from beginning to end. A conspiracy to discredit Washington’s administration and my office. Will you read them or do you condemn me to read them to you?

===Chinese===
**斯凯勒**：*（起身。如遭雷击）* 亚历山大！

**汉密尔顿**：父亲，我不作任何辩解。*（贾尔斯拿起信，快步过去递给杰斐逊看。杰斐逊别过脸去，连说「不，不。」贾尔斯又转向门罗。门罗接过信扔在桌上，说：「我不看。」汉密尔顿走回桌后）* 既有此指控，我就必须坚持请诸位阅看这全部信件。这些是他的——这些是她的。两者都是这场阴谋的组成部分，如今诸位自己也是这场阴谋里的人了。你们在信里找不到一个字提到州债。她的那些信，是想把我拖进一场旷日持久的私通，也是他敲诈要收足成效所必不可少的。*（望着站在门罗与杰斐逊之间的贾尔斯）* 从头到尾都是一场阴谋。一场败坏华盛顿政府和我的官署名声的阴谋。是你们自己读呢，还是非要我念给你们听？
===Original===
**Betsy**：*（Heard off L., calling.）* Alexander? *（A moment of suspense. Enters door L.）* Alexander! Oh, I beg your pardon, gentlemen, I thought you had gone. *（The men rise and there is an awkward pause.）* But this was to be my day with my husband. Won’t you let him off till tomorrow? We’ve been parted so long, you know. Won’t you, Mr. Jefferson?

**Hamilton**：*（Coming by L. of table to her rapidly.）* In a few moments, dear﻿—in a few moments, and then I’ll come.

**Betsy**：But you look so tired, dear﻿—so tired and old. I’ve never seen you look old before.

**Hamilton**：I’ve grown old in your absence, dear. I’ll come in a few moments﻿—very soon.

**Betsy**：And we’ll look at the shops﻿—and you’ll be my boy again.

**Hamilton**：*（Kisses her hand.）* Yes, dear.

===Chinese===
**贝特西**：*（左侧幕后传来呼声）* 亚历山大？*（片刻悬念。她自左门进来）* 亚历山大！哦，诸位先生，恕我失礼，我当你们已经走了。*（男人们纷纷起身，一时尴尬无语）* 可今天本该是我陪丈夫的日子呀。放他到明天不行吗？要知道，我们分别了好久好久。行吗，杰斐逊先生？

**汉密尔顿**：*（从桌子左边快步走到她身边）* 就一会儿，亲爱的——就一会儿，随后我就来。

**贝特西**：可你看上去那么疲惫，亲爱的——又疲惫又见老。我从没见你显过老态。

**汉密尔顿**：你不在的这些日子，我就老了，亲爱的。我就来——很快就来。

**贝特西**：我们一起去逛逛铺子——你就又是我那个小伙子了。

**汉密尔顿**：*（吻她的手）* 好的，亲爱的。

===Original===
**Jay**：*（Coming to L. C.）* I don’t think we need to ask Mrs. Hamilton to withdraw. Our business is over, and I apologize for having kept him so long. You came to take the reins, eh, Mrs. Hamilton? I think I will go before you use the whip. *（**Schuyler** has joined **Betsy** by this time. **Jay** draws **Hamilton** away to C.）* I sincerely regret that you should have been subjected to this.

**Betsy**：*（Down L. in front of table. By instinct.）* Is something the matter, father?

**Schuyler**：*（On her R. Tries to distract her attention.）* It’s all right, my dear; it’s all right.

**Jefferson**：*（Quietly, to **Hamilton**.）* Citizen Hamilton, I am heartily ashamed of having obtruded myself into this business. I give you my word that not one syllable of this shall ever pass my lips.

**Monroe**：I’m sorry. I was misinformed﻿ ﻿… I apologize.

===Chinese===
**杰伊**：*（走到中左）* 我想我们不必请汉密尔顿夫人回避了。我们的事已了，耽搁他这么久，我赔个不是。您是来接缰绳的吧，嗯，汉密尔顿夫人？我想我还是趁您没用鞭子之前先走为妙。*（此时斯凯勒已来到贝特西身边。杰伊把汉密尔顿拉到台中）* 让您经受了这一场，我由衷地过意不去。

**贝特西**：*（走到桌前左侧。凭直觉）* 出什么事了吗，父亲？

**斯凯勒**：*（在她右边，设法岔开她的心思）* 没事，亲爱的；没事。

**杰斐逊**：*（低声，对汉密尔顿）* 公民汉密尔顿，我竟搅进了这桩事里，实在惭愧得很。我向您保证，此事决不会有一个字从我嘴里漏出去。

**门罗**：对不起。是我误信了人……我赔罪。

===Original===
**Giles**：*（Who has been walking restlessly to and fro down R.）* Wait a minute, wait a minute! *（They all stop and turn. **Jefferson** at door, **Monroe** following. **Hamilton** C. and **Jay** C. to L. Up stage.）* I don’t want to speak before Mrs. Hamilton, but this thing hasn’t been brought to a satisfactory conclusion.

**Jefferson**：*（Coming back a few steps.）* Surely there is nothing more to be said.

**Jay**：Mr. Hamilton has met you fairly and straightforwardly.

**Giles**：*（R. C. in front of sofa.）* I have nothing to say against Mr. Hamilton, but﻿—

===Chinese===
**贾尔斯**：*（他一直在右前方焦躁地来回踱步）* 等一等，等一等！*（众人止步回身。杰斐逊已到门口，门罗随在后面。汉密尔顿在台中，杰伊在台中偏左靠上）* 我本不想当着汉密尔顿夫人的面讲，可这件事还没有个了结。

**杰斐逊**：*（回身走了几步）* 该说的想必都已说尽了。

**杰伊**：汉密尔顿先生待你们光明正大、坦坦荡荡。

**贾尔斯**：*（沙发前中右）* 我对汉密尔顿先生没什么可指摘的，不过——

===Original===
**Jay
						Jefferson
						Monroe**：Sh-sh!

**Giles**：*（Loudly and pugnaciously.）* I won’t be muzzled. I am a trusted representative of Virginia, and it is my duty to do the best for my state.

**Hamilton**：*（C.）* I think, gentlemen, we will not endeavor to restrain Mr. Giles in the execution of his duty.

===Chinese===
**杰伊
						杰斐逊
						门罗**：嘘——嘘！

**贾尔斯**：*（嗓门高大，气势汹汹）* 我不领受谁给我上笼头。我是弗吉尼亚受信赖的代表，为本州谋最大的利益是我的本分。

**汉密尔顿**：*（台中）* 诸位，我想我们就不必费心阻拦贾尔斯先生履行他的本分了。

===Original===
**Giles**：If Mrs. Hamilton will be good enough to retire﻿—

**Hamilton**：No, gentlemen, with your permission I shall ask my wife to remain.

**Giles**：Oh, I see, you want to gag me.

**Hamilton**：On the contrary, I am hoping that you will remove the seal from my own lips.

===Chinese===
**贾尔斯**：要是汉密尔顿夫人肯行个好，先回避一下——

**汉密尔顿**：不，诸位，恕我冒昧，我要请我的太太留下来。

**贾尔斯**：哦，我明白了，你是要堵我的嘴。

**汉密尔顿**：恰恰相反，我正盼着你把我嘴上的封条揭开呢。

===Original===
**Giles**：You mean that you want me to speak out?

**Hamilton**：*（Loudly and startlingly.）* Yes, I want you to speak out.

**Giles**：*（Slightly taken aback by his tone.）* Very well. *（Moves to C.）* Alexander Hamilton, you don’t like me, but I am going to prove to you that I am your friend. *（**Hamilton** glances at him.）* You don’t believe it, but I am going to prove it to you. What would you say if the newspapers got hold of this story?

===Chinese===
**贾尔斯**：你是说，你要我把话挑明？

**汉密尔顿**：*（声如惊雷）* 是的，我要你把话挑明。

**贾尔斯**：*（被他的口气微微镇住）* 很好。*（走到台中）* 亚历山大·汉密尔顿，你不喜欢我，可我要向你证明，我是你的朋友。*（汉密尔顿瞥他一眼）* 你不信，可我要证明给你看。倘若这事落到报纸手里，你会怎么说？

===Original===
**Hamilton**：Which﻿—my story or yours?

**Giles**：Now, it’s no use getting personal﻿—it isn’t my story, it’s Reynolds’. What would you say if the newspapers came out tomorrow with the story that the Secretary of the Treasury had borrowed the Nation’s money to speculate with﻿—to buy up the States’ paper?

**Hamilton**：*（Looking at his wife, who has started and is being restrained by **Schuyler**.）* For the moment, I don’t know what I should say, Mr. Giles.

**Giles**：It wouldn’t be any use trying to get your bill through then, not even with Jefferson and Monroe behind you. Public opinion would be too strong.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：哪件事——我的事，还是你的事？

**贾尔斯**：嗳，犯不着扯到私怨上头——这不是我的事，是雷诺兹的事。倘若明天报纸登出来，说财政部长挪用了国家的钱去搞投机——去收购各州证券，你会怎么说？

**汉密尔顿**：*（望向妻子。她惊得一颤，被斯凯勒扶住）* 眼下，我还不知道我会说什么，贾尔斯先生。

**贾尔斯**：到那时，你的州债法案就再想也别想通了，就算杰斐逊和门罗都站在你背后也没用。舆论会太厉害。

===Original===
**Jefferson**：*（Coming in between **Hamilton** and **Giles**.）* Citizen Hamilton, you must excuse me. I cannot stand by while Citizen Giles continues in this way. I wish to express my regret that a representative of Virginia should behave in such a deplorable manner. *（Bows to **Hamilton**.）* I take my leave.

**Giles**：*（Moving across to R. back angrily.）* Well, Jefferson’s words don’t alter facts﻿—at least not with me they don’t.

**Jay**：*（L. C. at back.）* Is there any danger of the newspapers getting it?

**Giles**：*（Coming to C.）* There’s more than a danger; it’s a fact that the *Advertiser* has got the story and is going to publish it in tomorrow’s issue, unless﻿—

===Chinese===
**杰斐逊**：*（走到汉密尔顿与贾尔斯中间）* 公民汉密尔顿，恕我失陪。公民贾尔斯再这样讲下去，我不能袖手旁观。一位弗吉尼亚的代表竟行止如此不堪，我深表遗憾。*（向汉密尔顿躬身）* 告辞了。

**贾尔斯**：*（愤然横穿到右后方）* 哼，杰斐逊的话改变不了事实——至少在我这儿改变不了。

**杰伊**：*（后方中左）* 这事真有落到报纸手里的危险吗？

**贾尔斯**：*（走到台中）* 何止是危险；《广告报》已经拿到这件事，明天一早就要见报，这是实打实的事——除非——

===Original===
**Hamilton**：*（In front of chair R. of table.）* Unless﻿—

**Giles**：I think it would be better if Mrs. Hamilton﻿—

**Hamilton**：Mrs. Hamilton is your hostess. You said, “unless”﻿—

**Giles**：Well, unless of course, you deny it, which you couldn’t very well because there are the proofs that the money was paid to Reynolds, and you can’t explain why.

**Hamilton**：*（After a pause and sitting.）* You’ve got me, haven’t you?

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：*（在桌右椅子前）* 除非——

**贾尔斯**：我想，汉密尔顿夫人要是——

**汉密尔顿**：汉密尔顿夫人是你的女主人。你方才说，「除非」——

**贾尔斯**：这个嘛，除非，当然喽，你出来否认——可你不大好否认，因为钱付给了雷诺兹，凭证俱在，你又解释不清缘由。

**汉密尔顿**：*（顿了顿，坐下）* 你们把我拿捏住了，是不是？

===Original===
**Giles**：Now, Mr. Hamilton, if you will withdraw your Bill, I’ll guarantee that the story shall not be printed.

**Betsy**：*（Crossing to **Hamilton**.）* I don’t know what foundation you have for your false accusations against Colonel Hamilton, but he must not give up that Bill. He’s worked and slaved, and gained the confidence of all the world with that in view, and he must not give it up!

**Hamilton**：Can you bear the alternative, Betsy?

**Betsy**：I must bear it.

===Chinese===
**贾尔斯**：这样吧，汉密尔顿先生，只要你撤回你的州债法案，我担保这件事决不会见报。

**贝特西**：*（走到汉密尔顿跟前）* 我不知道你凭什么对汉密尔顿上校横加不实之词，可他决不能放弃那项州债法案。他为了它操劳卖命，赢得了世人的信任，他决不能放弃！

**汉密尔顿**：另一个结果的滋味，你受得住吗，贝特西？

**贝特西**：受不住也得受。

===Original===
**Hamilton**：Can you bear that I, the Secretary of the Treasury, should be accused of cheating the people?

**Betsy**：Bear it? Why, of course, because I know it isn’t true.

===Chinese===
**汉密尔顿**：我，财政部长，被人指控欺诈人民——这个你受得住吗？
